TL;DR: 该论文利用297,915名学习者的自适应辅导历史数据，通过语义序变量和模型拟合定义了闭环人机系统中的高阶行为轨迹状态，发现了可复现的盆状流和亚稳态动力学，并提出了一个四条件条件机制来恢复群体漂移（r=0.946），同时验证了自监督学习、打乱训练和随机化等方法对状态流的影响，最终识别出一个外部锚定的引导级有效场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闭环人机系统产生的高维行为轨迹的集体动力学至今仍不清楚，需要一种方法来定义和解释这些复杂系统中的状态与演化模式。

Method: （1）在模型拟合前定义语义序变量，并在用户不相交的队列中进行测试；（2）定义状态的可复现盆状流和亚稳态样动力学，并用构造匹配的零模型区分归一化记忆弛豫与可复现的超额场；（3）提出四项条件机制恢复群体漂移；（4）使用预测级自监督学习恢复状态和学习的平面流；（5）采用打乱顺序训练和支撑对齐随机化来检验机制的结构特性。

Result: 四项条件机制恢复群体漂移的r=0.946（95% CI: 0.935-0.955）；跨模型共享引导级群体漂移r=0.866（95% CI: 0.857-0.875）；自监督学习能恢复状态和平面流，打乱顺序训练会使平面流反转，支撑对齐随机化选择性减少内向输运，且两个轴无需状态监督即可线性访问。

Conclusion: 研究发现了一个外部锚定的引导级有效场，它统一连接了经验动力学、可解释的机制和神经计算，为理解闭环人机系统的高维行为轨迹提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Closed-loop human-AI systems generate high-dimensional behavioural trajectories whose collective dynamics remain obscure. Using 297,915 learners' adaptive-tutoring histories, we define semantic order variables before model fitting and test them in user-disjoint cohorts. The state exhibits reproducible basin-like flow and operationally defined, state-heterogeneous metastable-like kinetics. A construction-matched null distinguishes normalised-memory relaxation from a reproducible excess field. A four-term conditional mechanism recovers population drift (r = 0.946; learner-bootstrap 95% CI, 0.935-0.955). Predictive event-level self-supervised learning recovers the state and learned-plane flow; null-referenced corrections retain directional, partial-amplitude excess-field structure without full calibration. Shuffled-order training reverses learned-plane flow on ordered trajectories; support-alignment randomisation selectively reduces inward transport. Both axes remain linearly accessible without state supervision. Without cross-model fitting, the models share leading population drift (r = 0.866; learner-bootstrap 95% CI, 0.857-0.875) and persistence ordering; residual directions remain model-specific. These results identify an externally anchored leading-order effective field linking empirical dynamics, an interpretable mechanism and neural computation.

</details>


### [168] [One Policy Is Enough: Single-Agent Reinforcement Learning Outperforms Tree Search for Chemistry Tool Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30952)
*Armin Dariani,Sifan Wu,Bang Liu,Entao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简化化学工具使用的方法：仅用单一策略模型，通过自左向右的交错推理、工具调用与返回生成，结合监督式预训练和基于程序化奖励的强化学习，在ChemToolBench基准上相比CheMatAgent的层次进化MCTS方法，在工具F1和返回F1指标上均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学问答需要精确计算和数据库查询，语言模型必须借助外部工具。现有工具使用框架复杂（如CheMatAgent使用层次进化MCTS，需要分离的策略和执行模型以及两个学习的critic），作者希望证明一个简单策略模型加上程序化奖励即可达到甚至超越复杂搜索方法的性能。

Method: 使用单一策略模型，以自左向右的方式交错生成推理、工具调用和工具返回内容；训练过程包含两个阶段：先用监督学习进行预热，再用基于程序化奖励的强化学习（奖励直接来自黄金调用链），无需学习型critic或裁判模型；推理时仅需一次模型调用，而CheMatAgent的搜索成本随树规模增长。

Result: 在ChemToolBench多种工具综合化学任务上：Qwen-2.5-7B上工具F1提升5.5%，返回F1提升9.6%；Llama-3.1-8B上分别提升3.7%和3.9%。同时在Qwen-2.5-7B上答案通过率也领先。

Conclusion: 单策略模型配合程序化奖励的强化学习足以取代复杂的层次进化MCTS与学习型critic方案，在化学工具使用任务上取得更优性能且推理成本更低，证明简化框架在该场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Chemistry questions often demand exact computation and database lookups that a language model cannot supply from its parameters, so it must reach for external tools. Tool use here is a three-part problem: select the right tool from a large pool, fill it with correctly typed arguments, and chain calls so that each consumes the outputs of the last. CheMatAgent, a previously published system, addresses this with hierarchical evolutionary MCTS: separate policy and execution models searching tool-call trees under two learned critics, one regressed partly onto GPT-assigned scores. We show that a single policy suffices. Our model interleaves reasoning, tool calls, and returns in one left-to-right generation, trained by a supervised warm-up and then outcome-level reinforcement learning against a programmatic reward read directly off the gold call chain, which leaves no learned critic and no judge in the training loop. On ChemToolBench multiple-tool comprehensive chemistry, on both backbones CheMatAgent use, we improve Tool F1 by 5.5% and Return F1 by 9.6% on Qwen-2.5-7B, and by 3.7% and 3.9% on Llama-3.1-8B, compared with their strongest search configuration, at one model invocation per question, against a search whose cost grows with the tree; we also lead answer Pass Rate on Qwen-2.5-7B.

</details>


### [169] [Singular Curvature in ReLU Training:Differentiation and the Gradient-Flow Limit Need Not Commute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30960)
*Xiaoyang Li,Runni Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究梯度下降（GD）与其连续时间极限——梯度流之间的导数差异。文章证明在固定非共振步长下，自动微分精确区分辨率离散GD程序，而离散导数收敛于一个事件无关的区域传播算子；相比之下，梯度流的导数包含归一化速度的激活事件转移。文章提出一个prepoint Stieltjes表示将绝对连续的Hessian与原子界面曲率分离，并证明了离散与连续导数之间的系统偏差，同时给出了可实现任意大灵敏度比的具体损失函数家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降作为梯度流的显式欧拉离散化，其连续时间替代模型在微分后可能不再保持精度。理解离散GD的精确导数（通过自动微分获得）与连续梯度流导数之间的差异，对于理论分析和实际应用（如超参数优化、灵敏度分析）都至关重要。

Method: 本文通过：(1) 在固定有限时域内证明GD状态的收敛性及精确离散导数向事件无关区域传播算子的收敛；(2) 引入prepoint Stieltjes表示将区域Hessian与原子界面曲率分离；(3) 构造残差ReLU平方损失风险家族展示任意大的倒数灵敏度比；(4) 将分解扩展到参数和反向模式伴随，并在标量或自治正则域中通过解析平滑和一致事件定位恢复流灵敏度。

Result: 证明了一个非零梯度跳跃产生精确的秩一端点差异，全局凸性阻止了严格事件的完全多事件抵消。同时构造出可实现任意大倒数灵敏度比的函数家族，具有统一的横截性裕度。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了离散GD导数与连续流导数之间差异的系统性刻画，明确了二者不等价的机制。但作者强调这是关于确定性全批量、有限时域、具有稳定有限事件序列的动力学的一致性定理，并非在大规模训练场景中的普遍性声明。

Abstract: Gradient descent (GD) is explicit Euler for gradient flow, but a state-accurate continuous-time surrogate need not remain accurate after differentiation. At every fixed nonresonant step size, ordinary automatic differentiation exactly differentiates the executed hard-ReLU GD program. We prove that, over a fixed finite horizon, the GD states converge and these exact discrete derivatives approach an event-free regional propagator, whereas the derivative of the limiting flow also contains speed-normalized activation-event transfers. A prepoint Stieltjes representation separates the absolutely continuous regional Hessian from atomic interface curvature; one nonzero gradient jump produces an exactly rank-one endpoint discrepancy, and global convexity prevents complete multi-event cancellation whenever an event is strict. Nevertheless, a standard family of globally 1-strongly convex residual-ReLU squared-loss risks realizes arbitrarily large reciprocal sensitivity ratios on open initialization sets, with a uniform transversality margin. The same discrete-versus-flow decomposition extends to parameters and reverse-mode adjoints; resolved smoothing in the scalar or autonomous-normal regime and consistent event localization recover the flow sensitivity. The results concern deterministic full-batch, finite-horizon dynamics with a stable finite itinerary of separated same-direction transverse events; they are consistency theorems, not prevalence claims for large-scale training.

</details>


### [170] [A Universal Context-Reuse Layer for Cross-Model KV Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30963)
*Yi Li,Dongming Jiang,Yi Zhao,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨模型KV共享（Cross-model KV Sharing）方法，将源模型的KV状态转换为目标模型可消费的表示，从而在异构LLM之间实现KV缓存复用，减少冗余的prefill计算。实验显示在多种跨模型、跨家族场景下均能显著降低推理成本并保持接近原生模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统中，多个模型处理相同输入时各自进行独立的prefill计算，造成大量冗余。已有的KV缓存复用机制假设生产者与消费者是同一模型，无法在不同模型之间共享KV状态，限制了缓存复用的适用范围。

Method: 提出跨模型KV共享机制，将源模型产生的KV状态通过翻译转换为目标模型可消费的表示。该方法不要求源模型与目标模型在规模、架构、注意力配置、分词器或模型家族上相同，适用于同族和跨族设置。

Result: Qwen2.5-7B→Qwen2.5-1.5B场景下准确率从27.59%提升至34.48%；跨族Qwen2.5-1.5B→Gemma-2-2B场景下目标端prefill成本降低67.05%；Llama3.1-70B→Qwen2.5-7B场景下准确率44.0%（原生45.7%），延迟从899ms降至138ms。

Conclusion: KV状态可以作为一种可迁移的计算表示，而非仅限于模型本地的缓存。作者提出"上下文移动性"（Context Mobility）作为系统抽象，以减少异构LLM和多代理推理工作流中的冗余prefill计算。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) serving systems increasingly operate over repeated or shared context, yet each model typically performs its own prefill computation even when another model has already processed the same input. Existing KV-cache reuse mechanisms substantially reduce redundant computation within a single model, but generally assume that the producer and consumer of a cache are identical. We study \emph{cross-model KV sharing}, which translates the KV state produced by a source model into a representation that can be consumed by a different target model, including models that differ in scale, architecture, attention configuration, tokenizer, and model family. We evaluate the approach in both within-family and cross-family settings. For Qwen2.5-7B $\rightarrow$ Qwen2.5-1.5B, translated KV states improve LongBench2 accuracy from 27.59\% to 34.48\%, a gain of 6.89 percentage points over the native 1.5B baseline, while reducing handoff cost relative to native target prefill. For the cross-family Qwen2.5-1.5B $\rightarrow$ Gemma-2-2B setting, KV handoff reduces target-side prefill cost by up to 67.05\% at 4K context length while maintaining decoding perplexity close to native-model baselines. In a more heterogeneous Llama3.1-70B $\rightarrow$ Qwen2.5-7B setting, cross-family handoff achieves 44.0\% accuracy compared with 45.7\% for native Qwen2.5-7B inference, while reducing measured latency from 899ms to 138ms. These results provide initial evidence that KV states can serve as transferable computational representations rather than strictly model-local caches, and motivate \emph{context mobility} as a systems abstraction for reducing redundant prefill across heterogeneous LLM and multi-agent inference workflows.

</details>


### [171] [A Human-in-the-Loop Autonomous Agent for Industry Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30976)
*Xiaoyu Tao,Mingyue Cheng,Ze Guo,Bokai Pan,Qi Liu,Shijin Wang,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CastClaw是一个基于人类参与的自主时间序列预测系统，通过面向预测的工程化设计（harness engineering）连接数据、专业模型、分析工具和用户输入。在五个电力价格数据集上，CastClaw在16个基线模型中取得了最低的点估计MSE和MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列预测不是一次性的模型调用，而是需要多步骤、多角色协作的复杂过程。专业预测模型虽然数值预测能力强，但通常运行在固定管道中，缺乏灵活性；而通用大语言模型智能体又缺乏预测领域特有的检查、约束和停止规则。因此需要一个将两者优势结合的预测系统。

Method: CastClaw采用人类参与（human-in-the-loop）的自主预测架构。用户以自然语言指定目标、预测范围、约束和假设。系统从提供的或模型生成的预测出发，检查时间模式和用户约束；当证据不足时，会检索上下文、运行分析或其他模型，或询问用户。在明确的停止条件下，系统决定保留、修订或升级结果。输出包含最终预测和执行报告，记录输入、证据、动作和修订过程。

Result: 在五个数据集的电力价格预测场景中，CastClaw在16个基线模型中取得了最低的点估计MSE和MAE。Nord Pool案例展示了可检查的工作流程。系统还在中国北方省份2026年1月至6月的省级电力负荷数据上进行了离线验证。

Conclusion: CastClaw通过将专业预测模型、分析工具和LLM智能体能力整合到一个带有人类反馈和显式停止条件的工作流中，实现了既精确又可检查的预测系统，验证了面向预测的harness工程化设计的有效性。

Abstract: Real-world time-series forecasting is rarely a one-shot model invocation: practitioners must formulate tasks, connect data and models, incorporate domain expertise, assess prediction plausibility, and communicate uncertainty. Specialized forecasting models provide strong numerical predictions but usually operate in fixed pipelines, while general-purpose large language model (LLM) agents often lack forecasting-specific checks, constraints, and stopping rules. We present CastClaw, a human-in-the-loop autonomous forecasting system built through forecasting-oriented harness engineering. CastClaw connects data, specialized models, analytical tools, user input, and a versioned execution record in one runtime. Users specify the target, horizon, constraints, and hypotheses in natural language. Starting from a supplied or model-generated forecast, CastClaw checks temporal patterns and user constraints; when evidence is missing, it retrieves context, runs an analysis or another model, or asks the user. It then keeps, revises, or escalates the result under explicit stopping conditions. The output contains the final forecast and an execution report recording inputs, evidence, actions, and revisions. In this five-dataset electricity-price setting, CastClaw reports the lowest point-estimate MSE and MAE among 16 baselines. A Nord Pool case demonstrates the inspectable workflow. CastClaw was also validated offline on provincial electricity-load data from North China covering January--June 2026.

</details>


### [172] [Sparse Competition during Training For the Emergence of Specialized Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30978)
*Baptiste Rossigneux,Karim Haroun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通过神经元组间竞争动态来诱导深度神经网络模块化的方法，在不使用模块级监督的情况下，在ImageNet-100和CIFAR-100上实现了专业化的功能模块自动涌现，各模块能响应语义类别（如狗或车辆），同时保持接近基线的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的模块化被认为能改善可解释性和训练效率，但如何自然地在标准架构中诱导模块结构并使其专业化仍是一个开放问题。作者希望通过竞争动力学实现无需模块级监督的模块自发涌现。

Method: 提出一种方法：(i) 保持接近基线的准确率；(ii) 通过稀疏路由将输入分配到神经元组，诱导基于使用频率的模块化；(iii) 鼓励模块专业化，使模块激活与输入类别相关。核心机制是神经元组之间的竞争动态。

Result: 在ImageNet-100和CIFAR-100上验证了该方法，表明无需模块级监督即可涌现专业化模块。单个模块能够响应语义类别（如狗或车辆），并观察到了依赖于模块数量的子任务层次性划分。

Conclusion: 竞争动态可以作为在标准架构中诱导功能模块化的简单机制，为深度神经网络带来更好的可解释性和结构理解。

Abstract: Modularity in deep neural networks has been proposed as a means of improving both interpretability and training by promoting disentangled representations and reducing redundancy. In this work, we study the emergence of modular structure through competition dynamics between groups of neurons during training. We introduce a method that (i) maintains near-baseline accuracy, (ii) induces usage-based modularity by sparsely routing inputs to neuron groups, and (iii) encourages specialization of these modules, such that their activations are correlated with input classes. We evaluate the proposed approach on ImageNet-100 and CIFAR-100 and show that with it, specialized modules emerge without module-level supervision. These modules capture a meaningful high-level structure in the data, with individual modules responding to semantic categories (e.g., dogs or vehicles). We also study the emergence of a hierarchical partition of sub-tasks depending on the number of modules. Our results suggest that competitive dynamics can serve as a simple mechanism for inducing functional modularity in standard architectures.

</details>


### [173] [Controlling Refusal Behavior of LLMs via Stiefel-Constrained Rotation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30986)
*Kirill Bunin,Dmitry Bylinkin,Vladimir Aletov,Daniil Medyakov,Vladimir Solodkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于黎曼优化的自包含旋转变换方法，用于大语言模型的激活引导（activation steering），无需依赖拒绝向量等辅助构建，即可在推理时高效控制模型拒绝行为，并通过实验验证了其干预效率的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的激活旋转干预技术依赖于拒绝向量等辅助构造来定义旋转，缺乏自包含性，且干预效率有限，需要一种更几何化、更可靠且不依赖外部辅助机制的控制方法。

Method: 基于黎曼优化（Riemannian optimization）学习参数高效的旋转变换，通过对激活空间进行可训练的旋转操作来实现对模型拒绝行为的干预，整个方法完全自包含，不依赖拒绝向量等外部构造。

Result: 实验验证了所提方法在干预效率上优于现有技术，并通过大量消融研究揭示了关键设计选择的重要性。

Conclusion: 基于旋转的引导方案为大语言模型行为提供更可靠的控制，是未来值得深入探索的研究方向。

Abstract: Activation steering has emerged as a lightweight approach for controlling model refusal at inference time. A growing line of research explores trainable rotations of activations to develop geometrically principled intervention mechanisms. However, existing techniques rely on auxiliary constructs, such as refusal vectors, to define these rotations. In our work, we develop a self-contained methodology for learning parameter-efficient rotational transformations based on Riemannian optimization. We empirically validate the proposed scheme, demonstrating its superiority in intervention efficiency. An extensive ablation study highlights the importance of key design choices in our method. Our results identify the proposed rotation-based steering scheme as a promising direction for more reliable control over the behavior of LLMs.

</details>


### [174] [Language-Informed Flow Matching for Trend-Guided Structure-Based 3D Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31009)
*Tianyu Gao,Zhikai Su,Jiashu Li,Wenjun Gao,Zichuan Ying,Zhe Zhao,Fei Zhang,Ye Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LiFT是一个基于Flow Matching的语言-跨模态框架，通过"感知-演化-组装"智能体生成目标感知的SMILES作为化学中间条件，提取语义先验并投影到3D几何生成中，实现无需额外微调的趋势引导3D分子生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控3D分子生成方法依赖任务特定的微调或采样时外部引导，成本高且可能与动态3D几何约束冲突。需要一种语言驱动的跨模态方法，在提供化学趋势引导的同时无需额外生成器微调。

Method: 提出LiFT框架：(1) Sense-Evolve-Assemble智能体生成目标感知SMILES作为中间化学条件；(2) 预训练化学基础模型提取连续语义先验；(3) 轻量语义投影器配合零初始化自适应归一化实现跨模态条件化；(4) 引入Self-Conditioned Decoupled Router (SCDR)在ODE积分过程中根据中间结构状态调制速度场。

Result: 在Cross-Docked2020数据集上，LiFT在任务引导设置下实现了有竞争力的分布匹配，同时改善了药物化学指标并保持竞争性的结构有效性，且无需额外生成器微调。

Conclusion: 语言衍生的化学先验能够为3D分子生成提供有效的趋势级引导，验证了跨模态语言信息在结构药物设计中的潜力。

Abstract: Structure-based drug design (SBDD) requires ligands that satisfy both 3D target affinity and 1D chemical validity. Existing controllable generation methods often rely on task-specific fine-tuning or externally imposed sampling-time guidance, adding cost and potentially conflicting with evolving 3D geometric constraints. We propose LiFT, a language-informed cross-modal framework built on Flow Matching for trend-guided 3D molecular generation across both de novo design and scaffold hopping. LiFT uses a "Sense-Evolve-Assemble" agent to generate target-aware SMILES as intermediate chemical conditions, from which a pre-trained chemical foundation model extracts continuous semantic priors. These priors are integrated into geometric generation through a lightweight semantic projector with zero-initialized adaptive normalization for stable cross-modal conditioning. We further introduce a Self-Conditioned Decoupled Router (SCDR), which modulates the velocity field according to intermediate structural states during ODE integration. Experiments on Cross-Docked2020 show that LiFT achieves competitive distribution matching while improving medicinal chemistry metrics and maintaining competitive structural validity under task-steering settings without additional generator fine-tuning. Our results suggest that language-derived chemical priors provide effective trend-level guidance for 3D molecular generation. Code and released artifacts are available at https://github.com/kasurl/LiFT.

</details>


### [175] [TSPFN: A Temporal Tabular Foundation Model for Physiological Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31013)
*Jérémie Stym-Popper,Clément Rambour,Federica Granese,Nicolas Thome,Olivier Bernard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TSPFN，一个将TabPFN架构重新设计用于时间序列数据的医学基础模型，通过整合结构化时间表征和位置嵌入来捕捉生理信号的时间与通道依赖，并在14万个真实生理时间序列上预训练，在多个生理基准上优于表格基线和TabPFN，跨领域泛化能力超越专门的深度时间序列模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学机器学习中低数据到中等数据场景下模型泛化仍是主要挑战，尤其是生理时间序列分类。虽然TabPFN等表格基础模型通过上下文学习提供了传统微调的替代方案，但它们无法捕捉生理信号固有的大量时间依赖关系。

Method: TSPFN重新设计了TabPFN的架构以适应时间序列数据，整合了结构化时间表征和位置嵌入来捕捉样本内的时间与通道依赖。模型在140,000个跨多个医学领域的真实生理时间序列上进行预训练，形成统一、可泛化的框架。

Result: 在多个生理基准上的实验表明，TSPFN一致优于标准表格基线和TabPFN，并且在跨领域泛化方面优于专门的深度时间序列模型。

Conclusion: TSPFN验证了将基础模型的上下文学习能力与时间序列结构感知相结合的有效性，为医学时间序列的低数据学习提供了可行的统一框架，代码与实验均已公开。

Abstract: Designing models that generalize effectively in low- to medium-data regimes remains a primary challenge in medical machine learning, particularly for physiological time-series classification. While tabular foundation models such as TabPFN offer an attractive alternative to conventional fine-tuning through in-context learning, they are not designed to capture the temporal dependencies inherent to physiological signals. ~In this paper, we introduce TSPFN, a foundation model that redesigns TabPFN's architecture for time series data. TSPFN integrates structured temporal representations and positional embeddings to capture intra-sample temporal and channel dependencies. To fully leverage its spatio-temporal design, the model is pretrained on 140,000 real-world physiological time series across multiple medical domains. This yields a unified, generalizable framework capable of learning the specificities of medical time series. Experiments across diverse physiological benchmarks demonstrate that TSPFN consistently outperforms standard tabular baselines and TabPFN, and achieves superior cross-domain generalization compared to specialized deep time-series models. All our experiments, ablation studies, and pre-processing scheme are publicly available at https://github.com/Jeremstym/TSPFN

</details>


### [176] [Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31036)
*Jiale Kang,Ziyin Yue,Zheng Zhan,Yangyi Huang,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NoRA（Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation），通过对LoRA下投影矩阵进行归一化来处理其训练动态，可在无需额外参数或推理计算的情况下加速收敛、提升性能和训练稳定性，并缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽被广泛用于参数高效模型适配，但其训练动态的规范化（正则化）问题尚未被充分探索。由于LoRA将上投影初始化为零，早期优化动态主要受下投影控制，利用这一观察可以改进LoRA的优化过程。

Method: 提出NoRA方法，在训练过程中对LoRA的下投影矩阵进行归一化。同时证明该归一化可仅应用于初始化阶段，无需在训练中反复归一化即可改进标准LoRA。

Result: 在预训练、监督微调和强化学习任务上，NoRA均能一致地加速收敛、提升性能与训练稳定性，并缓解灾难性遗忘。该方法不需要额外的可训练参数或推理时计算开销。

Conclusion: NoRA是一种简单且广泛适用的LoRA增强方法，通过归一化下投影矩阵显著改善了LoRA的训练动态与整体性能。

Abstract: While low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for parameter-efficient model adaptation, how to regularize its training dynamics for stable and effective optimization remains underexplored. Because LoRA initializes the up-projection to zero, its early optimization dynamics are largely governed by the down-projection. Building on this observation, we introduce Normalized Low-Rank Adaptation (NoRA), a simple yet effective method that normalizes the down-projection matrices during training. We further show that the same normalization can be applied only at initialization, improving standard LoRA without requiring repeated normalization throughout training. Across pretraining, supervised finetuning, and reinforcement learning, NoRA consistently accelerates convergence, improves performance and training stability, and mitigates catastrophic forgetting. These benefits require neither additional trainable parameters nor inference-time computation, making NoRA a simple and broadly applicable enhancement to LoRA.

</details>


### [177] [Rotational Equivariance in Machine Learning: A Comprehensive Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31045)
*Peter Lippmann,Fred A. Hamprecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本教程全面介绍了3D数据机器学习中的旋转等变性，从物理和几何直觉出发，系统讲解了群论、表示论、球谐函数、Wigner矩阵和Clebsch-Gordan分解等核心数学工具，并综述了群卷积、内部张量表示和规范化方法三大主流架构策略及其权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转对称性是3D数据机器学习的重要结构原理，预测结果不应依赖于任意坐标系的选取，但该领域涉及的数学知识门槛较高，且不同方法间缺乏统一的形式化框架，阻碍了研究者和从业者之间深入理解与选择。

Method: 通过教程形式系统搭建从物理直觉到数学工具的知识体系，涵盖欧氏图上的消息传递、群作用与表示、球谐函数、Wigner矩阵、张量积与Clebsch-Gordan分解，并综述群卷积、内部张量表示和基于规范化（canonicalization）的三类主要等变架构策略。

Result: 该教程将分散在不同领域的形式语言进行统一，明确了各类等变方法的数学原理、实践优缺点和适用范围，为从业者选择合适的方法提供了清晰的决策依据，降低了该领域的学习门槛。

Conclusion: 本教程旨在通过连接数学基础与实际模型设计，统一不同形式语言下的相关思想，并清晰地讨论不同方法的取舍，从而帮助研究者和实践者更好地理解和选用旋转等变深度学习架构。

Abstract: Rotational symmetry is one of the most important structural principles in machine learning on 3D data. In applications ranging from physics and materials science to 3D computer vision, predictions should not depend on an arbitrary choice of coordinate frame. Rotational equivariance captures this requirement mathematically by enforcing that a rotation of the input induces a corresponding transformation of the model output. This tutorial provides a comprehensive introduction to rotational equivariance, starting from the physical and geometric intuition behind coordinate independence and building up the necessary machinery from geometric deep learning, group theory, and representation theory. We introduce message passing on Euclidean graphs, group actions and representations, spherical harmonics, Wigner matrices, tensor products, and Clebsch-Gordan decomposition, and explain how these ingredients give rise to modern equivariant architectures. We then survey the principal strategies for incorporating rotational equivariance in deep learning, including group convolutions, internal tensorial representations, and canonicalization-based methods, and discuss their practical strengths and limitations. The tutorial aims to lower the barrier to the subject by connecting the underlying mathematics to practical model design, by unifying ideas that are often expressed in different formal languages, and by helping practitioners choose among competing approaches through a clear discussion of their trade-offs.

</details>


### [178] [Does On-Policy Distillation Really Distill? From Noisy Teacher to Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31046)
*Yi Ding,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑On-policy蒸馏（OPD）的有效性来源，发现其教师监督存在大量噪声，但学生策略对这些噪声不敏感。作者通过分析发现OPD的提升主要来自抑制低对数概率token，并不需要教师信号。基于此提出无监督的On-Policy Self-Adaptation (OPSA)方法，使用熵自适应负优势进行训练，在多个基准上显著优于OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD作为RLVR的替代方案提供密集的token级监督，但由于教师评分的是对教师而言off-policy的学生轨迹，其监督可靠性存疑。作者希望澄清OPD训练中教师监督的真实作用和学生的改进来源。

Method: 首先定量分析OPD训练中教师监督的噪声水平；然后通过消融实验（保留/移除噪声监督）分析学生策略对噪声的敏感性；进一步分析学习集中在低对数概率token的现象，验证使用固定负优势即可匹配教师提供的优势；基于上述发现提出OPSA方法，使用熵自适应负优势进行无监督训练。

Result: 发现教师监督噪声随教师规模增大而增加，但学生对其不敏感。OPD的增益主要来自抑制低对数概率token，不需要教师。OPSA在AIME24上Avg@32提升35.41点（相对提升263%），Pass@32在所有三个基准上翻倍以上，并在AIME24上比OPD高16.77点。

Conclusion: OPD本质上并非通过蒸馏教师的知识来提升学生，而是通过抑制低概率token实现的，这不需要教师参与。OPSA作为无监督方法比OPD更简单高效，在多个模型和任务上验证了有效性和泛化性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) offers dense token-level supervision as an alternative to the sparse outcome-level advantages of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). However, the teacher scores student-generated trajectories that are inherently off-policy for it, so the reliability of its supervision, and hence the source of the student's improvement, remains unclear. We quantitatively analyze teacher supervision during OPD training and find substantial noise whose prevalence increases with teacher scale. Surprisingly, the student policy is insensitive to such noise, converging to comparable performance regardless of whether noisy supervision is retained or removed. Does OPD distill at all? By analyzing what drives its gains, we find that learning concentrates on low log-probability tokens, and using a single fixed negative advantage matches the performance of teacher-provided ones. This suggests that OPD works largely by suppressing low log-probability tokens, which requires no teacher. These findings motivate On-Policy Self-Adaptation (OPSA), a supervision-free method using entropy-adaptive negative advantages. It assigns stronger learning signals to high-entropy positions, suppressing tail tokens, and evenly redistributing probability mass among head tokens. Compared with the base \texttt{Qwen3-1.7B}, OPSA improves Avg@32 by 35.41 points on AIME24, corresponding to a 263\% relative gain, and more than doubles Pass@32 across all three benchmarks. It also outperforms OPD by 16.77 points in Avg@32 on AIME24. Extensive experiments and analyses across model families and tasks further demonstrate its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [179] [Universal Transformers for Circuit Computations: Perfect Length Generalization in Tiny Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31067)
*Takuya Ito,Ruchir Puri,Murray Campbell,Parikshit Ram*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可证明正确的Transformer参数化方法（仅需280个可学习参数），能够以O(n·d)的复杂度精确学习和评估任意深度或长度的布尔代数运算，并在模块化算术和ListOps等长度泛化基准上达到100%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络在组合泛化和长度泛化基准上持续失败，学习通用算法计算仍然是一个重大挑战。

Method: 将算法任务概念化为嵌入Transformer中的电路模型，通过深度感知的位置编码跟踪每个门的深度，利用掩码硬注意力识别可求值的子表达式，结合线性注意力实现O(n)每轮复杂度，并通过自主停止准则在d轮后终止。采用浅层（深度1和2）样本训练即可恢复可解释的参数。

Result: 在布尔代数任务上仅需280个可学习参数，训练浅层实例即可精确泛化到任意长度；在模块化算术和ListOps等其他长度泛化基准上达到100%准确率。

Conclusion: 该Transformer变体通过电路化设计和深度感知机制，能够实现可证明的精确长度泛化，为解决神经网络的算法泛化问题提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Learning generalizable algorithmic computations remains a challenge for neural networks, as reflected in persistent failures on compositional and length generalization benchmarks. We present a provably correct, transformer parameterization (with only 280 learnable parameters for Boolean algebra tasks) capable of learning and evaluating problems of any depth or length. We assume inputs are fully parenthesized, well-formed expressions. Our approach conceptualizes algorithmic tasks as circuit models embedded in transformers, enabling depth-1 circuit reduction in a single forward pass. To achieve depth generalization, we introduce a positional encoding that tracks each gate's depth within the circuit, enabling the model to identify evaluable subexpressions at each iteration via masked hard attention, with $O(n)$ per-iteration complexity via linear attention. Combined with an autonomous halting criterion, the model terminates after $d$ iterations for problems of depth $d$, yielding $O(n \cdot d)$ total complexity. We show that training on shallow problem instances (depth 1 and depth 2) effectively recovers interpretable parameters that {\em snap} into place, resulting in exact length generalization. Though we establish that our construction provably evaluates Boolean expressions -- a universal symbolic computation -- of arbitrary length perfectly, in other experiments we also demonstrate that our transformer variant can learn and generalize perfectly (100% accuracy) on other common length generalization benchmarks, including modular arithmetic and ListOps.

</details>


### [180] [A Model with No Head and Many Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31069)
*Nikita Koriagin,Yaroslav Aksenov,George Bredis,Gleb Gerasimov,Nikita Balagansky,Daniil Gavrilov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Soft Latent Thinking方法，用轻量级投影器替代LM头，在嵌入空间中实现连续自回归推理，避免每个步骤解码到离散token的高昂计算成本。在DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B和LLaMA-3.2-3B上，该方法在所有k值下均提升pass@k，同时减少思维链推理中每步的计算量，并取得所有软思考方法中最高的pass@32。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在每一步推理时都需要将隐藏状态投影到庞大的词表头上进行解码，这一操作计算成本高昂，且迫使所有推理过程以离散token形式表达，限制了推理的连续性和效率。

Method: 在推理阶段用轻量级投影器替换LM头，使模型能够在嵌入空间中进行自回归展开，推理步骤保持连续而非token化，从而避免离散解码的开销。

Result: 在DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B和LLaMA-3.2-3B两个模型上验证，Soft Latent Thinking在所有k值下均提升pass@k指标，同时减少了思维链推理中每个步骤的计算量，并在所有软思考方法中取得了最高的pass@32成绩。

Conclusion: 有效推理可以在连续空间中完成，无需生成离散token，Soft Latent Thinking证明了以连续隐空间方式进行推理既可行又高效。

Abstract: Large language models decode by projecting hidden states through a large vocabulary head at every step. This operation is computationally costly and forces all reasoning to be expressed in discrete tokens. We introduce Soft Latent Thinking, a method that replaces the LM head during reasoning with a lightweight projector, enabling autoregressive rollout in embedding space where reasoning steps remain continuous rather than tokenized. Experiments on DeepSeek-Qwen-1.5B and LLaMA-3.2-3B show that Soft Latent Thinking consistently improves pass@k across all k while reducing per-step compute during chain-of-thought. Our method achieves the highest pass@32 among all soft-thinking approaches, demonstrating that effective reasoning can be carried out in continuous space without discrete token generation.

</details>


### [181] [Sycophantic Agreement Transfers with Neutral Data via Contrastive Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31079)
*Camila Blank,Zhuofan Ying,Christopher Potts,Peter Hase,Jing Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的谄媚附和行为（sycophantic agreement）可能作为对比性偏好优化目标（如DPO等7种目标）的意外后果而出现。通过OLMo 3后训练流程，研究发现教师模型的谄媚率对数比与学生模型的谄媚率高度相关，且该信号扩散在整个数据集而非集中在少数样本中，简单的数据筛选方法无法有效缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 谄媚附和是语言模型对齐中已知的失败模式，但对它在模型训练过程中如何产生缺乏深入理解。理解这一机制对于改进对齐训练和减少有害行为至关重要。

Method: 使用OLMo 3后训练流程，对比三个模型家族的多种教师模型对，分析教师模型谄媚率的对数比与学生模型谄媚率之间的相关性；验证该效应在DPO及另外6种偏好优化目标中是否普遍存在；通过探针数据归因和logit线性选择等方法分析偏好数据中谄媚信号的分布特征。

Result: 教师模型的谄媚率对数比与学生模型谄媚率存在强相关性，该转移效应在7种偏好优化目标中均出现；谄媚信号并非集中在少量样本中，而是扩散在整个数据集上，每个样本表面上都显得中性；基于探针归因或logit线性选择的数据筛选方法都需要移除大量数据才能缓解谄媚问题。

Conclusion: 偏好数据生成所用的教师模型与对齐训练目标之间存在未预期的交互作用，可能导致谄媚附和等不良行为的泛化。这提示研究者需要重新审视偏好优化目标与教师模型选择之间的相互作用。

Abstract: Sycophantic agreement refers to a behavior in which language models excessively affirm the user, often at the cost of factual accuracy. Although sycophantic agreement is a well-known failure of model alignment, there is limited understanding of how it emerges from model training. In this work, we demonstrate that sycophantic agreement can emerge as an unintended consequence of widely used contrastive preference optimization objectives. Using the OLMo 3 post-training pipeline, we show that, for various pairs of teacher models across three families, there is a strong correlation between the log-ratio of the teacher model sycophantic agreement rates and the resulting student model sycophantic agreement rate. We further demonstrate that this unintended transfer is not limited to DPO but also occurs across 6 other preference optimization objectives. To understand whether this effect can be attributed to particular training examples, we analyze the preference data and find that the sycophancy signal is diffused across the entire dataset rather than concentrated in a sparse set of examples: each example appears neutral, i.e., there are no explicit instances of sycophantic agreement, and filtering based on probe-based data attribution or logit-linear selection fails to mitigate sycophancy without removing a large portion of the dataset. Overall, our findings suggest that the teacher models used to generate preference data can interact with alignment training objectives in unexpected ways, generalizing to undesirable and potentially harmful behaviors like sycophantic agreement.

</details>


### [182] [Stress-Testing Efficient Responsible-AI Evaluation: When Compute Savings Change Benchmark Conclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31108)
*Ahmed El Kady,Aravind Narayanan,Rehana Noorani,Yani Ioannou,Shaina Raza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对负责任AI基准测试中的高效评估方法进行压力测试，考察三种稠密和混合专家模型在BBQ和BBQ-V上的结论稳健性，涵盖批处理、量化、基准缩减及其组合等七种条件。研究发现较大批处理基本保持结论稳定并降低能耗，INT8量化保留质量但能耗升高，INT4引发较大且依赖情境的变化，缩减基准节能最稳定但对子集选择高度敏感。研究主张将高效评估视为一种测量干预，必须针对基准旨在支持的各项结论进行有效性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效评估手段被广泛用于降低评测成本，但其对模型行为结论的稳定性鲜有检验——评估方法本身可能改变结论，因此有必要系统性地压力测试高效评估在负责任AI基准中的结论稳健性。

Method: 在BBQ和BBQ-V基准上，对三个稠密和混合专家模型施加七种评估条件：批处理、INT8/INT4量化、基准缩减及组合。除聚合准确率外，还比较偏差严重度与普遍性、推理质量、子群行为、子集成员稳定性、运行时间和GPU能耗，并以全基准BF16为基线对照。

Result: 较大批处理使准确率保持在基线0.35个百分点内且子群变化较小，在六个模型-数据集组合中有五个降低能耗；INT8基本保留质量但能耗为基线1.79-4.26倍；INT4导致更大且依赖模型与情境的变化；缩减基准节能最一致，但极小子集对保留条目高度敏感。

Conclusion: 高效评估应被视为一种测量干预，其有效性必须针对基准所支持的全部结论维度（而非仅聚合准确率）进行验证，以确保评估效率提升不以牺牲结论稳健性为代价。

Abstract: Efficient evaluation changes the protocol used to support claims about model behavior, yet it is rarely tested whether those claims remain stable after the evaluation itself is made cheaper. We stress-test conclusion robustness in responsible-AI benchmarking by evaluating three dense and mixture-of-experts models on BBQ and BBQ-V under seven conditions spanning batching, quantization, benchmark reduction, and their combinations. Rather than treating preserved aggregate accuracy as sufficient, we compare accuracy, bias severity and prevalence, reasoning quality, subgroup behavior, subset-membership stability, runtime, and measured GPU energy against a full-benchmark BF16 baseline. Larger batching keeps accuracy within 0.35 percentage points of baseline and produces comparatively small subgroup changes, while reducing energy in five of six model--dataset settings. INT8 largely preserves quality but uses 1.79--4.26$\times$ baseline energy. INT4 causes larger, model- and context-dependent changes. Reduced benchmarks provide the most consistent savings, but very small subsets are substantially more sensitive to which items are retained. Efficient evaluation should therefore be treated as a measurement intervention whose validity must be checked across the conclusions the benchmark is intended to support. Our project website is https://vectorinstitute.github.io/sustainable-rai-evaluation/ and the code is available at https://github.com/VectorInstitute/sustainable-rai-evaluation.

</details>


### [183] [On the Complexity of the Compatibility Problem for Succinctly Encoded Conditional Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31120)
*Guy Emerson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了概率模型中的兼容性问题（compatibility problem）在简洁表示下的计算复杂度。当条件分布以算术电路（arithmetic circuits）这一简洁形式编码时，兼容性判断变得困难：非零概率情形下为co-NP-complete，允许零概率时多种版本为PSPACE-complete。此外，若多项式层级不坍缩，存在兼容但无法被简洁表示的联合分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习中常使用条件概率进行预测，但一对条件分布p(x|y)和p(y|x)未必与任何联合分布p(x,y)兼容。现有的兼容性问题求解方法仅适用于以概率表编码的离散随机变量，而在高维场景（如神经网络模型）中，条件概率以简洁形式（如算术电路）编码，使得原有方法不再适用。

Method: 将条件分布编码为算术电路，形式化定义了简洁版的兼容性问题；分别考虑所有概率非零和允许零概率两种情况；通过复杂度归约证明了该问题在非零概率情形下为co-NP-complete，在零概率情形下多个版本为PSPACE-complete；并利用多项式层级不坍缩的假设证明了存在兼容但联合分布无法简洁表达的例子。

Result: 对于算术电路表示的简洁条件分布，兼容性问题在非零概率情形下是co-NP-complete，在允许零概率的情形下多个版本是PSPACE-complete；在多项式层级不坍缩的假设下，存在兼容的简洁条件分布，但其联合分布无法被简洁表示。

Conclusion: 简洁表示的兼容性问题的计算复杂度远高于概率表表示的情形，这对概率建模和机器学习具有重要启示：在高维简洁建模场景中，验证条件分布是否兼容是计算上困难的，且兼容的联合分布可能本身无法简洁表达。

Abstract: The motivation for this paper is the investigation of the trade-offs implicit in probabilistic models used in machine learning. Models are often used to make predictions in the form of conditional probabilities. However, a pair of conditional distributions p(x|y) and p(y|x) may not be compatible with any joint distribution p(x,y). Given two such conditionals, determining if there exists a compatible joint is known as the compatibility problem. For discrete random variables, when the conditionals are encoded as probability tables, the compatibility problem has a known solution, which is computationally tractable. In this paper, we formalise and study a succinct version of the problem, encoding conditional distributions as arithmetic circuits. This is applicable to practical applications of probabilistic modelling in high-dimensional settings, including neural network models. We show that, for succinct circuit representations of conditionals, the compatibility problem is intractable. In the case that all probabilities are non-zero, the problem is co-NP-complete. In the case that probabilities can be zero, we give examples to demonstrate that several notions of compatibility can be distinguished, and we prove that multiple versions of the problem are PSPACE-complete. Furthermore, we show that, assuming the polynomial hierarchy does not collapse, there exist compatible succinct conditionals whose joint cannot be expressed succinctly. Implications of these results for probabilistic modelling and machine learning are discussed.

</details>


### [184] [Sharp Approximation Rates for Neural Networks with Affine Latent Parameterizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31157)
*Shijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究参数生成框架（如超网络）中，隐空间维度M与网络参数预算P之间的权衡。对于仿射生成器和全连接ReLU网络，作者证明在α-Hölder函数类上，最优最坏情况逼近误差的阶为(P·min{M,P})^{-α/d}，表明即使固定隐空间维度也能随网络预算增加而逼近误差趋零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效方法（超网络、低维参数化、参数高效适配、模型压缩）的共同核心问题是：如何在低维隐表示与网络表达力之间取得平衡。理解隐维度M与网络预算P的权衡关系是刻画这些方法表达效率的基础，但目前缺乏严格的理论刻画。

Method: 作者在仿射生成器G和全连接ReLU架构的框架下，联合优化架构Φ（满足P_Φ≤P）和仿射生成器G:R^M→R^{P_Φ}，分析在[0,1]^d上α-Hölder函数单位球上的最坏情况均匀逼近误差。

Result: 证明了最优最坏情况均匀逼近误差的精确阶为(P·min{M,P})^{-α/d}。该结果表明即使隐空间维度M固定（不随P增长），只要P增加，逼近误差仍可趋于零，即固定维度隐空间足以实现渐近完备逼近。

Conclusion: 该工作为参数生成框架的表达效率提供了严格的理论上界与下界，揭示了隐维度与网络预算之间的定量权衡关系，对理解超网络等参数高效方法的理论基础具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Many parameter-efficient methods generate the parameters of a large neural network from a low-dimensional latent representation. Given an architecture $Φ$ with $P_Φ$ parameter slots, we write $\boldsymbolθ_f=\mathcal{G}(\boldsymbolξ_f)$, where $\mathcal{G}\colon\mathbb{R}^M\to\mathbb{R}^{P_Φ}$ is a parameter generator and $\boldsymbolξ_f\in\mathbb{R}^M$ is a latent representation of the target function $f$. The architecture $Φ$ and the generator $\mathcal{G}$ are shared across the entire target class, while each target $f$ is represented by its own latent vector $\boldsymbolξ_f$, with $Φ_{\mathcal{G}(\boldsymbolξ_f)}$ approximating $f$. This framework encompasses hypernetworks, low-dimensional parameterizations, parameter-efficient adaptation, and model compression. Understanding the tradeoff between the latent dimension $M$ and the network budget $P$ is therefore fundamental to characterizing the expressive efficiency of these methods. We study this tradeoff for affine generators and fully connected ReLU architectures. More precisely, optimizing jointly over architectures $Φ$ satisfying $P_Φ\leq P$ and affine generators $\mathcal{G}:\mathbb{R}^M\to \mathbb{R}^{P_Φ}$, we prove that the optimal worst-case uniform approximation error over the unit ball of $α$-Hölder functions on $[0,1]^d$, where $0<α\leq1$, has the sharp order $ \bigl(P\min\{M,P\}\bigr)^{-α/d}. $ In particular, our result shows that even a fixed-dimensional latent space suffices to achieve vanishing approximation error as the network budget increases.

</details>


### [185] [Constant Individual Regret in General Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31166)
*Mingyang Liu,Gabriele Farina,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ECHO-OFTRL算法，通过EMA级联实现高阶乐观，彻底去除了有限N人正规型博弈中每个个体悔恨对时间视界T的多对数依赖，使单次悔恨上界仅与参与人数和动作集大小相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往无耦合的无悔动态虽能提供通往均衡的去中心化路径，但个体悔恨保证中包含对时间视界的多对数依赖项，这在长视界下不够理想。作者希望消除这种依赖，实现与视界无关的悔恨上界。

Method: 提出ECHO-OFTRL算法，即乐观跟随正则化领导者（OFTRL）搭配EMA（指数移动平均）级联来实现高阶乐观。该算法受现代滤波器设计启发，采用确定性的完全无耦合机制，能在所有有限N人正规型博弈下运行。

Result: 对于每个时间视界T≥1，在最大动作集大小为m_max的N人有限正规型博弈中，每位玩家的悔恨上界为O(poly(N, log m_max))，即与视界T完全无关。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在完整信息反馈下为有限正规型博弈实现了完全无耦合且与视界无关的个体悔恨保证，其高阶乐观思想受滤波器设计启发，为无耦合动态理论提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Uncoupled no-regret dynamics provide a decentralized route to equilibrium, but prior guarantees for individual regret retain a polylogarithmic dependence on the horizon. We remove this dependence for every finite $N$-player normal-form game under full-information feedback. We introduce \emph{ECHO-OFTRL}: optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OFTRL) equipped with an EMA cascade for high-order optimism (ECHO), where EMA denotes exponential moving average. The algorithm is deterministic and fully uncoupled. If $m_{\max}$ denotes the largest action-set size, then, simultaneously for every horizon $T\geq1$, it guarantees that each of the $N$ players in the game incurs regret upper bounded by $O(\textrm{poly}(N, \log m_{\max}))$. Our algorithm leverages a new form of optimism inspired by modern filter design.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [186] [DS-Lighting: Making Agent Harnesses Explicit for Data-Science Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28590)
*Fan Liu,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DS-Lighting，一个统一的数据科学自动化智能体框架工具包，将智能体框架显式分解为数据、工作流、执行和评估四个可复用层次，并将多个开源基准整合为统一的MLE-Bench风格任务格式，以提升可复现性、可比性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据科学智能体通常将框架（harness）隐式化，导致结果难以复现、比较和归因。框架设计对端到端性能至关重要，但缺乏统一的显式设计方案。

Method: DS-Lighting将框架解构为四个可复用层（数据、工作流、执行、评估），将多样智能体表示为可执行操作符程序，支持预定义流水线和自适应搜索，并整合多个开源基准为统一的MLE-Bench风格任务格式，提供共享任务接口、沙盒运行时和度量协议。

Result: 在智能体、框架、模型和消融实验上的实验表明，显式框架设计能够提升可复现性、可比性和可靠性，同时减少端到端数据科学工作流中可避免的系统级故障。

Conclusion: 显式化框架设计对于数据科学自动化具有重要意义，DS-Lighting通过系统化的框架解构提升了智能体的整体性能和工程可用性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents have shown promise for automating data-science workflows, yet their end-to-end performance depends critically on the agent harness that represents tasks, manages execution state, constrains output artifacts, and provides evaluation feedback. Existing data-science agents often leave this harness implicit, making results difficult to reproduce, compare, and attribute across heterogeneous tasks. We introduce DS-Lighting, a unified harness toolkit that makes harness design explicit for data-science automation. DS-Lighting decomposes the harness into four reusable layers: data, workflow, execution, and evaluation, and represents diverse agents as executable operator programs that support both predefined pipelines and adaptive search. We further integrate multiple open-source data-science benchmarks into an MLE-Bench-style task format, enabling controlled comparison under a shared task interface, sandboxed runtime, and metric protocol. Experiments across agents, harnesses, models, and ablations show that explicit harness design improves reproducibility, comparability, and reliability, while reducing avoidable system-level failures in end-to-end data-science workflows. Our code is available at https://github.com/usail-hkust/dslighting

</details>


### [187] [Expert-validated STEM QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28591)
*Kihwan Han,Saurabh Patil,Chinmayee Shukla,Abhinav Sharma,Marko Pavlovic,Anshuman Lall,Mahesh Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个由241位领域专家创建的"专家验证STEM问答数据集"（N=398），覆盖物理、化学、生物和数学四个学科。该数据集针对现有STEM数据集的四大缺陷（模型性能饱和、分类分布不均、多选题格式与现实不符、答案和解析不准确）进行了系统性改进。研究表明，前沿AI模型在该数据集上性能低于25%，而在该数据集私有版本（N=2000）上进行后训练可将开源模型在HLE验证STEM子集上的性能相对提升15%（p=0.045）。部分数据集已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的STEM评估数据集存在多个缺陷：模型性能饱和导致评估无区分度、分类分布不均衡、多选题格式与科学家实际使用AI的方式不一致，以及基于竞赛和限时评审导致答案和解析不准确。这些缺口限制了AI在STEM领域的进一步发展和可靠评估。

Method: 研究者采取了四项关键措施：（1）精心设计均衡分布的分类体系；（2）通过质量驱动激励机制筛选问题贡献者；（3）进行多轮评审，基于专家共识验证修改；（4）采用可验证的问答格式构建数据集。数据集由241位领域专家创建，覆盖物理、化学、生物学和数学四个学科。

Result: 前沿AI模型在该数据集上的性能低于25%，表明数据集具有足够的难度和区分度。在该数据集的私有版本（N=2000）上进行后训练后，开源模型在HLE验证数据集的STEM子集上相对基线模型性能提升了15%（p=0.045），证明了数据集在模型训练方面的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该研究通过专家验证和严格的质量控制流程构建了高质量的STEM评估数据集，解决了现有数据集的关键缺口，既可用于基准测试，也可用于模型训练。部分数据已开源供AI研究社区使用。

Abstract: Recent advancements in AI are helping scientists achieve breakthroughs in fields such as mathematics, medicine, and materials sciences. New evaluation datasets for AI models contribute to such advancement in AI. In the STEM domain, frontier models have consumed most of the available online data, creating the need for human-created datasets that codify the knowledge of leading experts in the domain. There are several STEM datasets available for the research community in this field. However, there are some gaps in these datasets, leaving room for improvement. Examples of gaps include (1) saturation in model performance on these datasets, leaving no head-room for meaningful evaluations, (2) skewed taxonomy distributions, (3) multiple choice question format that is misaligned with how scientists use AI in the real world, and (4) inaccurate answers and rationales partially led by a contest-based data collection and a time-bound review process. In this study, we present 'Expert-validated STEM QA', a high-quality, expert-validated STEM dataset (N=398) in Physics, Chemistry, Biology, and Mathematics, created by 241 domain experts. We (1) carefully designed a taxonomy with balanced distribution, (2) vetted question contributors with quality-driven incentive, (3) conducted multiple rounds of reviews with revisions validated by domain experts based on consensus, and (4) created the dataset in verifiable question and answer format. Our study demonstrated low performance ($<25\%$) of frontier AI models on the dataset as a benchmark. Post-training on a separate, private version of the dataset (N=2,000) increased performance of the open source model by $15\%$ relative to the baseline model (p=0.045) on the STEM subset of HLE-verified dataset, indicating potential utility of the dataset for model training. We have open-sourced a portion of our dataset for the AI research community.

</details>


### [188] [A collective capability boundary in frontier large language models on guideline-conformant and case-specific oncology decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28592)
*Zhang Sheng,Jinming Li,Wangyang Chen,Zhiwei Bao,Yu YoSean Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了肿瘤决策边界基准（ODBB），包含2,005个肿瘤决策点，评估了2025年6月至2026年4月间发布的九个前沿大语言模型。结果显示42.1%的条目没有任何模型答对，且决策盲点集中在指南路径选择本身，两个专为决断力调优的模型出现不安全承诺的频率是谨慎模型的3-5倍但不更高分。作者认为模型质量不再是临床部署的主要瓶颈，关键在于需要架构级干预来检测模型能力边界并路由给临床医生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学benchmark主要衡量事实性知识回忆，但真实肿瘤学是路径选择、升级判断和不确定性承诺的序列决策。作者质疑前沿LLM是否存在组合模型也无法修复的决策路径盲点，并希望量化这一盲点。

Method: 构建ODBB基准（2,005个决策点，涵盖NCCN指南和结直肠癌病例），采用零LLM推理的确定性评分器将输出分类为14种失败类型，由两名肿瘤学家独立验证（加权κ=0.939和0.790）。评估九个前沿LLM（4个闭源、5个开源权重系列），将其视为合并的超级模型进行分析。

Result: 42.1%的条目（NCCN 35.7%，结直肠癌66.4%）没有任何模型答对；失败集中在路径选择前的元判断。GPT-5.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview等决断力调优模型不安全承诺频率是谨慎模型的3-5倍但未获得更高分数。3-9%的条目中模型陈述了正确步骤但未承诺执行——这是决策失败而非知识失败。

Conclusion: 模型质量不再是临床LLM部署的主要瓶颈；真正的约束是假设任何单一模型可以成为临床决策的唯一基础。未来需要能检测模型能力边界并在超出时路由给临床医生的架构，而非仅增加训练数据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve high scores on medical knowledge examinations, yet real-world oncology is not a knowledge test--it is a sequence of guideline-pathway choices, escalation judgments, and commitments under uncertainty. Existing benchmarks largely measure factual recall, leaving open whether frontier LLMs share decision-path blind spots that combining models cannot fix. We built the Oncology Decision Boundary Benchmark (ODBB)--2,005 oncology decision points across NCCN guidelines and colorectal cancer cases--and evaluated nine frontier LLMs (four closed-source, five open-weight families) released between June 2025 and April 2026. A fully deterministic scorer (zero LLM inference) classified outputs into 14 failure types, independently validated by two oncologists (Cohen's weighted $κ$ = 0.939 and 0.790) on a 225-item stratified sample. Treating the nine as a pooled super-model, 42.1% (Wilson 95% CI 40.0--44.3%) of all items--35.7% of the 1,586 NCCN items and 66.4% of the 419 colorectal-cancer cases--were answered correctly by none, with failures concentrated in choosing between guideline pathways before reasoning within any: a consistent blind spot in clinical meta-judgment that likely requires architectural intervention rather than more training data. Two models tuned for decisiveness (GPT-5.5, Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview) made unsafe commitments three to five times more often than the seven cautious models without scoring higher. In 3--9% of items, models stated the correct next clinical step yet did not commit to it--failures of decision, not knowledge. Model quality is no longer the primary bottleneck for clinical LLM deployment; the binding constraint is the assumption that any single model can be the sole basis for a clinical decision. Progress requires architectures that detect when a model reaches its competence boundary and route the decision to a clinician.

</details>


### [189] [Statutory AI: Aligning Large Language Models With Legal Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28593)
*Cindy Delage,Stéphane Canu,Marc Décombas,Jonathan Foureur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Statutory AI混合方法，利用现有法律条文作为AI系统的宪法框架，通过两阶段Chain-of-Thought提示让AI自主审查和修正输出，实验表明其在减少有害内容方面比Constitutional AI高出约10个百分点，同时计算时间降低50%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI对齐方法存在局限：Constitutional AI依赖人类监督，而GfH等广义规范框架过于笼统和模糊，难以提供可操作性的治理指导，需要一种更具体、可执行的AI对齐方案。

Method: 提出Statutory AI混合方法，以法律语料中特定主题的既有原则作为宪法框架。采用两阶段Chain-of-Thought提示：第一阶段将用户提示分类到识别的主题中，第二阶段结合该主题法律语料中的相关条款进行分析和修订输出。

Result: 在1,000个红队提示和五个刑法主题（歧视、泄露机密信息、暴力、欺诈、虐待弱势群体）的实验中，Statutory AI将有害内容减少52至59个百分点，比标准Constitutional AI高出约10个百分点，同时计算时间减少超过50%。

Conclusion: Statutory AI通过利用法律条文作为约束框架，能在不依赖人类监督的情况下实现更有效的AI对齐，兼具可操作性和效率优势，为AI治理提供了新的可行路径。

Abstract: With the increasing development of AI regulatory frameworks, ensuring that artificial intelligence systems, particularly generative models, operate in accordance with legal and ethical standards has become a critical priority. Existing proposals for AI alignment and value-guided behavior, however, face some limitations. Approaches such as Constitutional AI depend on human supervision, while broad normative frameworks like the Good-for-Humanity (GfH) principle may be overly general and ambiguous to provide actionable governance guidance. To overcome these limitations, we propose a hybrid approach called Statutory AI that employs pre-existing human-authored principles drawn from specific themes within a legal corpus. Specifically, Statutory AI uses legal texts as a constitutional framework, enabling AI systems to autonomously critique and revise their outputs according to established norms. It operates in two stages, both using Chain-of-Thought prompting. The first stage classifies the user prompt into one of the identified themes, while the second stage analyzes it in conjunction with relevant articles selected from the legal corpus of that theme. To illustrate the potential of our approach, we conducted an experiment involving 1,000 red-teaming prompts and five penal themes: discrimination, disclosure of confidential information, violence, fraud, and abuse of vulnerable persons. Statutory AI reduced harmful content by 52 to 59 percentage points across tested models, approximately 10 percentage points higher than standard Constitutional AI, while cutting computation time by over 50%.

</details>


### [190] [From Question-First to Analyst-First: Domain-Expert Skills and Verified Knowledge Compilation for Proactive Enterprise Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28594)
*Harmohit Singh,Rahul Sharma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种反转交互范式的生产级分析系统：从"问询优先"变为"分析师优先"，通过可插拔的领域专家"技能"抽象和离线知识编译循环，在用户使用查询框之前就主动生成经过验证的报告与建议问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话分析系统假设用户已具备清晰的问题，导致非专家面对空白查询框和陌生企业模式时无从下手。商业'主动式'工具仅能通过分析师管理的指标层检测统计异常，学术界的下一步问题推荐器依赖新数据集所缺乏的查询日志。

Method: 两个核心架构思想：(1)可插拔的领域专家'技能'抽象——基于文件夹、无数据库的主题包（清单、分阶段提示面、关键词路由引用、报告模板、可选计算），通过确定性模式匹配自动选择，(2)离线知识编译循环——智能体通过DuckDB探测parquet数据，运行带批评门控的逐表收敛与自愈重试，并通过值重叠验证join，产出持久的模式知识。

Result: 提供了形式化模型和说明性单租户证据。明确声明不做用户研究或基准测试声明，贡献在于架构本身及其可辩护性。

Conclusion: 该系统通过报告产生数字、数字产生问题、点击启动经过验证的深度探索，在查询框被使用之前就关闭了主动循环。技能作为自包含文件夹确定性解析，使目录开放可扩展，形成可扩展的领域专家市场。

Abstract: Conversational analytics systems assume the user already has a well-formed question, leaving a non-expert facing a blank query box on an unfamiliar enterprise schema. Commercial 'proactive' tools narrow this gap only by detecting statistical anomalies over analyst-curated metric layers, and academic next-question recommenders depend on query logs that a fresh dataset lacks. We describe a production analytics system that inverts the interaction model from question-first to analyst-first through two coupled architectural ideas. First, a pluggable domain-expert 'skill' abstraction: a folder-based, database-free subject-matter pack (a manifest, per-stage prompt facets, keyword-routed references, report templates, and optional compute) auto-selected per (client, dataset) by deterministic schema matching and spliced as a cross-cutting concern into every stage of an agentic pipeline, the schema explorer, and the report engines, degrading to a strict no-op when absent. Because a skill is a self-contained folder resolved deterministically, the catalogue is open-ended: an extensible marketplace of domain experts. Second, an offline knowledge-compilation loop: an agent probes the dataset's parquet via DuckDB (zero load on production), runs critic-gated per-table convergence with self-healing retries, and data-validates joins by value overlap, producing durable schema knowledge that drives standing expert reports whose every published metric is re-verified by re-executing its evidence SQL, plus suggested questions that mirror the report agenda. These close a proactive loop: reports surface numbers, the numbers seed questions, and a click launches a verified deep dive, all before the query box is used. We give a formal model and report illustrative single-tenant evidence. We make no user-study or benchmark claims; the contribution is the architecture and its defensibility.

</details>


### [191] [The Signal in the Noise: An Auditable Reliability Layer for Biomedical Text Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28595)
*Moustafa Yehia Hassan,Sharon Wong,Woh Kai Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种保守、可审计的生物医学NLP拼写校正可靠性层，在不确定时主动放弃编辑（遵循"无害"原则），通过有限编辑距离候选生成、语料驱动的n-gram评分和生物医学安全门控，在CORD-19三分类任务上恢复了约80.45%的噪声导致性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学NLP流水线通常预设干净的输入文本，但大规模语料库经PDF自动解析后充斥着OCR伪影、分词/合并、连字符残留和字符级损坏等问题，系统性侵蚀词汇证据并降低下游分类器性能。现有校正方法追求最大准确率，可能对领域关键术语造成伤害，缺乏安全性和可审计性。

Method: 设计了一个确定性架构：采用有界编辑距离候选生成，结合语料库衍生的n-gram评分，并配备一套生物医学安全门控以保护领域关键术语。系统在不确定条件下主动放弃编辑（abstain），体现"医学无害"哲学。评估采用内在基准（2,104个token级案例）和外在四运行协议（Clean, Noisy, Restored, Safety），外加103篇真实OCR摘要的BioBERT案例研究。

Result: 内在评估中，合成错误上的error-fix召回率达94.61%，负控制上零有害编辑。下游实验中，将宏F1从Noisy的0.7654提升至Restored的0.7717（恢复约80.45%的噪声损耗），Safety模式达0.7721接近干净性能。真实OCR案例研究表明Transformer编码器对轻度噪声相对鲁棒。

Conclusion: 该确定性、工件驱动的可靠性层在保证完全可审计性的前提下有效恢复噪声数据带来的性能损失，未来可集成有界神经信号和UMLS词表构建灰盒架构，在不牺牲可审计性的前提下进一步提升鲁棒性。

Abstract: Biomedical NLP pipelines routinely presuppose clean input text, yet large-scale corpora assembled through automated PDF parsing harbour pervasive OCR-like artifacts, token splits and merges, hyphenation remnants, and character-level corruption, that systematically erode lexical evidence and degrade downstream classifiers. We introduce a conservative, fully auditable spell-correction reliability layer conceived as a safety-oriented preprocessing module rather than a maximal-accuracy corrector: under conditions of uncertainty, the system abstains from editing, in accordance with a medical do-no-harm philosophy. The deterministic architecture couples bounded edit-distance candidate generation with corpus-derived n-gram scoring and a suite of biomedical safety gates that protect domain-critical terminology. We evaluate the layer both intrinsically, on a manually curated benchmark of 2,104 token-level cases, and extrinsically, on a tri-class CORD-19 topic classifier (Prevention, Treatment, Epidemiology) spanning 10,000 examples under a principled four-run protocol (Clean, Noisy, Restored, Safety). Intrinsically, the layer attains 94.61% error-fix recall on synthetic errors with zero harmful edits on negative controls. Downstream, it recovers approximately 80.45% of the noise-induced macro-F1 degradation, elevating macro-F1 from 0.7654 (Noisy) to 0.7717 (Restored) while preserving near-clean performance (Safety: 0.7721). A supplementary case study on 103 real-world OCR-extracted abstracts classified with BioBERT confirms that transformer encoders appeared relatively robust to mild noise, motivating a future grey-box architecture that integrates bounded neural signals and UMLS lexicons without compromising auditability. The system is fully deterministic, artifact-driven, and designed with deployment and auditability in mind.

</details>


### [192] [Paper Pilot: A Human-in-the-Loop Expert System for Evidence-Traceable Scientific Manuscript Generation in Applied Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28596)
*Nidhi Jha,Siddharth Chaudhary,Ajinkya Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Paper Pilot，一个用于科学论文生成的人类在环专家系统，通过审批门控、证据锁定、声明分类和审计日志等机制，确保AI辅助写作中的观点、方法和结果可溯源至已批准的文献或实验证据。初步实验表明，该系统在引用生成任务中将虚构引用率从25%降至0%，并能显式标记证据缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体虽能推进自主科学发现和稿件生成，但当思想、方法、结果和声明在AI辅助工作流中传播时，缺乏强制性人工审批和制品级可追溯性，导致治理问题——AI生成的引用和声明可能无法追溯到真实证据。

Method: 基于协作智能体推理工程（CARE）方法学，在稿件开发中引入：稿件所有者审批门控、显式不通过标准、声明分类、审计日志、LLM咨询审查和证据锁定修订控制。定义从想法到声明的八道审批门，区分文献依据型声明和制品依据型声明，要求数字和解释必须追溯到已批准证据。

Result: 在受控基准测试中（两个商业LLM、真实arXiv论文、无LLM评判者），在覆盖率压力下，无门控的草稿者虚构了高达25%的引用且从未标记证据缺口；而在Paper Pilot的证据锁定规则下，同一模型产生零虚构引用，并将植入的缺口以显式占位符呈现。结果接地、修订和对抗鲁棒性的初步结果方向一致。

Conclusion: Paper Pilot将LLM辅助写作定位为受控的人机协同决策过程而非完全自主的作者流水线。系统提示词已公开释出，可部署于ChatGPT、Gemini、Claude或机构LLM环境。完整评估留给未来工作。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly embedded in scientific workflows for literature analysis, drafting, and review. Existing systems advance autonomous discovery and manuscript generation, but do not resolve the governance problem that arises when ideas, methods, results, and claims propagate through AI-assisted workflows without mandatory human approval or artifact-level traceability. This paper proposes Paper Pilot, a human-in-the-loop expert system for evidence-traceable scientific manuscript generation in applied sciences. It adapts the Collaborative Agent Reasoning Engineering (CARE) methodology to manuscript development through manuscript-owner approval gates, explicit no-pass criteria, claim classification, audit logging, advisory LLM review, and evidence-locked revision control. The framework defines eight approval gates across the idea-to-claim pipeline and distinguishes literature-grounded from artifact-grounded claims, requiring reported numbers and interpretations to remain traceable to approved evidence; its system prompt is openly released for deployment in ChatGPT, Gemini, Claude, or institutional LLM environments. As a first empirical validation, we evaluate the citation-grounding layer with a controlled, mechanically scored benchmark (two commercial LLMs, real arXiv papers, no LLM judge): under coverage pressure ungated drafters fabricated up to 25% of their citations and never flagged an evidence gap, whereas the same models under Paper Pilot's evidence-locked rules produced zero fabricated citations and surfaced the planted gaps as explicit placeholders. Preliminary results for result grounding, revision, and adversarial robustness point the same way; full evaluation is left to future work. Paper Pilot positions LLM-assisted writing as a controlled human-AI decision-support process rather than a fully autonomous authorship pipeline.

</details>


### [193] [The Race between Agentic AI Capabilities and Data Quality Control in Online Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28597)
*Sourav Panda,Hillmer Chona,Rupak Kumar Das,Shreyash Kale,Shikha Soneji,Jonathan Dodge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了智能体AI架构能否完成在线调查问卷并通过注意力检查（attention checks）。研究发现，单智能体架构可通过结构化解析利用DOM元数据泄露和可预测的选项编码等漏洞绕过注意力检查；作者提出了DOM元数据混淆的防御策略，并评估了多种开源语言和多模态模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线调查广泛依赖注意力检查来保障数据质量，但智能体AI的快速发展对这些安全机制提出了新的挑战。需要系统性地评估智能体AI架构是否能够绕过这些检查，以及如何设计相应的防御措施。

Method: 构建一个受控的调查沙箱环境，采用单智能体架构（具备多模态输入处理和基于工具的网络交互能力）来完成问卷。从攻击视角分析结构化漏洞（如DOM元数据暴露、可预测选项编码）；从防御视角实施DOM元数据混淆策略。评估多个开源语言和多模态模型的能力与编排效果。

Result: 证明了智能体AI能够利用结构化解析方式解析出DOM元数据和选项编码，从而成功通过注意力检查；DOM元数据混淆策略可以有效去除文本问题中的语义线索，降低攻击成功率；不同开源模型在能力和协作编排效果上存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究者需要同时考虑经验主义数据收集需求与智能体AI研究的需求，文章为如何在两个方面取得平衡提供了见解，包括评估攻击面、实施防御措施，并为未来在线调查工具的设计提供了方向。

Abstract: Online surveys are a foundational data collection instrument in a variety of fields, with attention checks serving as critical guardians of response quality. However, the rapid emergence of agentic AI (goal directed systems powered by a large language model (LLM) brain and/or a multimodal processing unit with tool-augmented capabilities) raises new questions about the robustness of these safeguards. We investigate how well agentic AI architectures can complete web-based surveys and pass standard attention checks. We evaluate a single-agent architecture capable of multimodal input processing and tool-based web interaction on a controlled survey sandbox. We analyze the problem from two perspectives. From an attack perspective, we demonstrate how structural vulnerabilities such as exposed DOM metadata and predictable option encoding allow agents to resolve attention checks through structured parsing only. From a defense perspective, we implement a mitigation strategy of DOM metadata obfuscation to remove semantic cues in text-based questions. We evaluate multiple open-source language and multimodal models to study capability and orchestration effectiveness. Based on our evaluations, we offer perspectives on how to simultaneously meet the needs of empiricists and agentic AI researchers.

</details>


### [194] [CDPR: Counterfactual Advantage-based Credit Assignment for Cost-Aware Sequential Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28599)
*Qi Peng,Yi Cai,Changmeng Zheng,Xin Wu,Jiayuan Xie,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CDPR方法，将临床诊断建模为代价敏感的序贯决策过程，通过强化学习训练策略，利用反事实诊断过程奖励解决信用分配问题，在多个基准上同时提升诊断准确性并降低检查数量与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学语言模型将诊断视为一次性分类任务，忽略了真实临床诊断中逐步检查、观察结果并更新诊断的序贯过程，也未考虑检查价值与成本的权衡。此外，序贯决策中的信用分配困难使得效率低下的检查过程与高效的检查过程得到相同评分。

Method: 将诊断建模为代价敏感的序贯决策过程并用强化学习训练策略；提出CDPR（反事实诊断过程奖励），无需专家标签和学习的批评器。CDPR首先利用策略动作分布的不确定性找出策略犹豫的状态，然后通过短轨迹回放，在平衡正确性、检查次数、成本和不可行请求的效用函数下，估计所选动作相对于策略自身会考虑的其他备选动作的优势。使用回放缓存复用小批量内轨迹以降低计算成本，并将CDPR集成到GRPO中。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV（域内）及ClinicalBench和私有医院数据集（域外）三个基准上进行测试，CDPR在提升诊断准确率的同时，显著减少了检查次数和检查成本。

Conclusion: CDPR通过反事实奖励机制有效解决了序贯诊断中的信用分配问题，实现了诊断准确性与检查成本之间的更优平衡，且在域内外数据上均表现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis is a step-by-step, cost-aware process: a physician orders examinations one at a time, observes the results, and updates the diagnosis before reaching a final conclusion. Most medical language models instead treat diagnosis as a one-pass classification task and ignore the trade-off between a test's value and its cost. We model diagnosis as a cost-aware sequential decision process and train the policy with reinforcement learning. The main difficulty is credit assignment: the only reliable signal comes once at the end of a long trajectory, so it scores a wasteful workup the same as an efficient one. We propose CDPR (Counterfactual Diagnostic Process Reward), which needs no expert labels and no learned critic. CDPR first finds the states where the policy hesitates, using the uncertainty of its action distribution, and then scores the chosen action by its advantage over the alternatives the policy itself would consider, estimated with short rollouts under a utility that balances correctness against test count, cost, and infeasible requests. A rollout cache reuses within-batch trajectories to keep the cost low. We integrate CDPR into GRPO and test it on one in-domain (MIMIC-IV) and two out-of-domain (ClinicalBench and a private hospital dataset) benchmarks. CDPR improves diagnostic accuracy while clearly reducing the number and cost of examinations.

</details>


### [195] [SHAPE of Chain-of-Thought in Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28600)
*Jonghyun Song,Sangjun Song,Minjae Oh,Haesung Pyun,Sungsik Lee,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SHAPE框架，从数学教育学的两个视角（语义空间与启发式策略）分析大语言模型在数学推理中的思维链（CoT）轨迹，发现启发式策略比传统CoT特征更能解释答案正确性，并基于此进行后训练优化以提升数学推理准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在数学推理基准上表现优异，但其底层数学推理技能尚未得到充分研究，缺乏一个理论扎实的诊断框架来理解和改进模型的数学推理过程。

Method: SHAPE框架从两个视角分析CoT轨迹：(1)语义空间——模型对问题不断演化的数学解释（如代数、几何）；(2)启发式策略——在特定语义空间中采取的具体数学行为（如简化问题、逆向推导）。利用该框架分析多种模型的推理模式，并据此设计后训练方法促进多样化启发式策略的使用。

Result: 研究发现：(1)模型使用的数学启发式策略比传统CoT特征更能解释最终答案的正确性；(2)模型在少数语义空间中集中推理努力时更可能得到正确答案，这与人类行为模式一致；(3)强化学习后训练会诱导启发式使用中的模式趋同（mode-seeking）；(4)通过促进多样化启发式策略进行后训练可有效提升模型准确率。

Conclusion: SHAPE提供了一个基于理论的诊断框架，用于解码LLM的数学推理过程，并为数学推理后训练开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve strong performance on mathematical reasoning benchmarks, yet the mathematically meaningful skills underlying their reasoning remain underexplored. We introduce \texttt{SHAPE}, a framework that analyzes Chain-of-Thought (CoT) trajectories through two lenses developed in mathematics education: (1) semantic spaces: the model's evolving mathematical interpretations of a problem (e.g., algebraic, geometric), and (2) heuristics: the specific mathematical actions taken within those spaces (e.g., simplifying the problem, working backward). We first use \texttt{SHAPE} to analyze the reasoning patterns of various models. Our findings reveal that the mathematical heuristics employed by a model better explain final answer correctness than traditional CoT features. Furthermore, models are likely to reach correct solutions by concentrating their reasoning effort within a few semantic spaces rather than exploring many disparate ones -- a pattern consistent with human behavior. Next, we utilize the \texttt{SHAPE} lens to evaluate whether post-training truly enhances mathematical proficiency. We find that reinforcement learning induces mode-seeking in heuristic usage. Lastly, we post-train LLMs by promoting diverse heuristics and demonstrate its effectiveness in improving accuracy. Overall, \texttt{SHAPE} provides a theoretically-grounded diagnostic framework for decoding LLM reasoning and offers a new path toward post-training LLMs for math reasoning. The code for our model is available at https://github.com/holi-lab/SHAPE-of-CoT

</details>


### [196] [Leveraging Generative AI to Design Accessible Interactive Visualizations for Undergraduate Mathematics: A Six-Phase Workflow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28601)
*Mahesh Sunkula,Kuan-Hua Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个六阶段工作流（Foundation, Customization, Mathematical Depth, Application, Accessibility, Pedagogical Control），利用生成式AI帮助缺乏编程技能的教师构建符合WCAG 2.2 AA标准的大学数学交互式可视化工具，无需编写代码。通过设计型研究，在四个部署工具上验证了该工作流的前向与后向可行性，但强调每个阶段仍需人工验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式可视化能促进大学数学的本科理解，但构建此类工具需要编程专业知识，而大多数教师不具备这一技能，因此需要一种无需编程的解决方案。

Method: 采用设计型研究方法，开发并部署一个六阶段工作流，利用生成式AI构建可视化。每个阶段结构化提示、引导AI代码生成，并定义人工验证点。在前向（从自然语言想法生成工具）和后向（从成品工具逆向推导提示）两个方向上进行评估。

Result: 在涵盖微积分、多元微积分和微分方程的四个部署工具上，评估了数学正确性（对照闭式解）、可访问性（自动化与人工屏幕阅读器测试）及反复出现的错误。结果表明工作流能生成结构完整的一次性工具，但每个阶段都必须进行人工验证（数学正确性、可访问性和教学适配性）。

Conclusion: 该六阶段工作流是平台无关的，既能服务教师也能服务学生，能可靠地生成可访问的教学可视化工具，但人工验证在每个阶段都是不可替代的强制环节。

Abstract: Interactive visualizations support conceptual understanding in undergraduate mathematics, but building them has required programming expertise most instructors lack. Using a design-based research approach, we develop, deploy, and evaluate a six-phase workflow (Foundation, Customization, Mathematical Depth, Application, Accessibility, Pedagogical Control) that uses generative AI to build WCAG~2.2 Level~AA compliant visualizations without programming. The six phases structure every prompt, scaffold the AI's code generation, and define where human verification is applied. We ask whether the structure reliably yields correct and accessible tools, whether it runs both backward (reverse-engineering prompts from a finished tool) and forward (generating a tool from a plain-language idea), and what verification each phase requires. Across four deployed tools spanning calculus, multivariable calculus, and differential equations, we evaluate mathematical correctness against closed forms, accessibility through automated and manual screen-reader testing, and the errors that recurred. The structure produces structurally complete first-pass tools, but human verification remains mandatory at every phase: each output must be checked for mathematical correctness, accessibility, and pedagogical fit before the next phase begins. The workflow is platform-independent and serves both instructors and students.

</details>


### [197] [Integrating Triaxial IMU Sensors and Ensemble Learning for Effective Parkinson Disease Severity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28602)
*Rehan Khan,Muhammad Junaid Asif,Rana Fayyaz Ahmad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用可穿戴惯性测量单元（IMU）采集运动和震颤数据，结合多种机器学习分类模型来检测帕金森病的对比系统。实验结果表明LightGBM模型在所有评估指标上表现最佳，准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数均达到约97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帕金森病是一种进行性神经退行性疾病，严重影响患者运动功能和生活质量，早期准确识别对临床治疗和疾病管理至关重要。近年来可穿戴传感器技术和人工智能的发展使得非侵入性、数据驱动的疾病检测方法成为可能，本文旨在利用IMU数据构建有效的PD检测系统。

Method: 使用IMU采集加速度计和陀螺仪在X、Y、Z三个方向上的运动与震颤信号数据，比较了七种机器学习分类模型（SVM、LR、KNN、DT、XGBoost、LightGBM）在帕金森病检测中的效果。

Result: Logistic Regression在各评估指标中表现约75%，KNN约90%，SVM约94%，决策树和XGBoost接近96%，而LightGBM表现最佳，准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数均达到约97%。

Conclusion: 提出的基于机器学习的方案在帕金森病严重程度分类中展现出准确且有效的预测能力，LightGBM模型具有最优的分类性能。

Abstract: Parkinson disease PD is a progressive neurodegenerative disease that can have a significant impact on motor performance resulting in the appearance of symptoms such as tremors rigidity postural instabilities and bradykinesia. Timely clinical treatment disease management and quality life of the patients are closely linked to early and appropriate identification of PD. Over the past few years the growth of wearable sensor technology and artificial intelligence AI have made it possible to create noninvasive and data driven disease detection methods. This paper proposes a comparative system using artificial intelligence to detect Parkinsons disease by analyzing the motion and tremor data captured by an inertial measurement unit IMU. The data comprises the signals of the acceleration and gyroscope sensors measuring movement in three directions X Y and Z. The signs and symptoms provide helpful information about subtle motor deficits associated with PD. Several classification models like Support Vector Machine SVM Logistic Regression LR KNearest Neighbors KNN Decision Tree DT Extreme Gradient Boosting XGBoost and Light Gradient Boosting Machine LightGBM were used to compare their effectiveness. The Logistic Regression model had a performance around 75 percent in all evaluation metrics and KNearest Neighbours KNN around 90 percent. The support vector machine SVM performed almost 94 percent whereas the performance of classifiers such as Decision Tree and XGBoost was close to 96 percent and overall classification efficacy respectively. LightGBM model performs consistently at the best rank among all of the evaluated methods having Accuracy, Precision, Recall and F1score of around 97 percent. The results show that the proposed machine learning approach offers an accurate and effective predictive capability in the classification of PD severity.

</details>


### [198] [C3-UniMM: Causal Cycle-Consistent Unified Multimodal Modeling via Super Alignment and Shared Decoding Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28603)
*Yujie Shen,Lianlei Shan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C3-UniMM，一种基于因果循环一致性与超对齐的统一多模态框架。通过引入结构化潜在因果图（SLCG）作为共享跨模态语义空间，并设计统一解码空间来保证结构保持和语义可逆性，解决了现有统一多模态模型缺乏跨模态结构一致性约束导致的语义漂移、组合泛化差和干预不稳定性等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型主要依赖隐式统计相关性建模，缺乏跨模态结构一致性约束，导致语义漂移、组合泛化能力差以及干预条件下的不稳定性等深层问题。

Method: 提出C3-UniMM框架：1）引入结构化潜在因果图（SLCG）作为跨模态共享语义空间；2）设计统一多模态编码块，使理解与生成在同一因果语义结构中协同优化；3）提出统一解码空间，在跨模态生成过程中强制执行结构保持与语义可逆性。

Result: 理论分析证明该框架显著增强了跨模态映射的可逆性和机制不变性。在多项理解、生成和组合泛化任务上的大量实验表明，C3-UniMM大幅优于现有统一多模态基线方法。

Conclusion: C3-UniMM通过因果循环一致性与超对齐机制，为统一多模态建模提供了结构化的语义约束，有效解决了传统方法在语义一致性和泛化性方面的不足。

Abstract: Unified Multimodal Models aim to achieve any-to-any understanding and generation across arbitrary modalities. However, existing methods primarily rely on modeling implicit statistical correlations and lack cross-modal structural consistency constraints. This deficiency leads to profound issues, including semantic drift, poor compositional generalization, and instability under interventions. In this paper, we propose C3-UniMM, a unified multimodal modeling framework based on Causal Cycle Consistency and Super Alignment. Specifically, we introduce a Structured Latent Causal Graph (SLCG) as a shared cross-modal semantic space and design unified multimodal encoding blocks, enabling understanding and generation to be synergistically optimized within the identical causal semantic structure. Furthermore, we propose a Unified Decoding Space to enforce structural preservation and semantic invertibility during the cross-modal generation process. Theoretical analyses demonstrate that our approach significantly enhances both the invertibility and mechanism invariance of cross-modal mappings. Extensive experimental results across multiple understanding, generation, and compositional generalization tasks indicate that C3-UniMM substantially outperforms existing unified multimodal baselines.

</details>


### [199] [MedTVL: Harnessing Vision and Language for Medical Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28605)
*Jiexia Ye,Jia Li,Fugee Tsung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedTVL，一种文本引导的双路径架构，用于医学时间序列分类任务。该架构通过卷积时序路径和Transformer视觉路径分别捕捉数值序列的细粒度动态和时间序列图像的形态结构，并引入文本语义引导和Mixture-of-Experts机制进行实例级自适应融合，同时支持多模态对比学习以缓解标签稀缺问题。实验在多种医学数据集和多任务设置下验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学时间序列分类方法主要局限于双模态交互（如时序+文本），缺乏对时序、视觉和语言三模态协同潜力的探索；同时，现有方法未充分考虑跨模态和架构异构性带来的互补性，以及标签稀缺在临床应用中的挑战。

Method: 构建MedTVL双路径架构：（1）基于卷积的时序路径处理原始数值序列以捕捉细粒度时间动态；（2）基于Transformer的视觉路径处理时序衍生图像以捕捉整体形态结构；（3）两条路径由自适应医学文本语义进行引导以解决诊断歧义；（4）Mixture-of-Experts机制为每个实例动态路由到专门的融合专家，捕捉实例级别的模态依赖；（5）支持多模态对比学习缓解标签稀缺问题。

Result: 在多个医学数据集和任务上（包括监督学习、少样本学习和对比学习设置）的系统实验表明，MedTVL在性能和可迁移性方面均优于现有方法，展现了其在稳健临床决策支持中的潜力。

Conclusion: MedTVL通过时序、视觉和语言三模态协同以及跨模态和架构异构性的结合，为医学时间序列分类提供了更全面的诊断视角，同时在标签稀缺场景下通过对比学习保持有效表现，具有临床决策支持的广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Recent advancements in multimodal learning for medical time series (MedTS) classification highlight the benefits of integrating complementary modalities for clinical decision. However, existing methods typically focus on bi-modal interactions (e.g., time series and text), leaving the tri-modal synergy between time series, vision, and language largely unexplored. Inspired by diagnostic practice synergizing numerical assessment, visual inspection and clinical context, we introduce MedTVL, a text-guided dual-pathway architecture tailored for MedTS classification. Specifically, it synergizes a convolution-based temporal pathway for fine-grained temporal dynamics from raw numerical sequences and a transformer-based visual pathway for holistic morphological structures from time-series-derived images. Such combination of cross-modal and architectural heterogeneity provides a comprehensive diagnostic perspective. To further resolve potential diagnostic ambiguity, both pathways are guided by adaptive medical textual semantics. Finally, a Mixture-of-Experts mechanism dynamically routes each instance to specialized fusion experts, capturing instance-specific reliance on the temporal and visual pathway outputs. In addition, MedTVL supports multimodal contrastive learning to mitigate the clinical label scarcity challenge. Extensive experiments across multiple medical datasets and tasks, spanning supervised, few-shot, and contrastive learning settings, demonstrate the superiority and transferability of MedTVL, highlighting its potential for robust clinical decision support.

</details>


### [200] [RegDivergence-101: An LLM Benchmark for Cross-Jurisdiction Regulatory Contradiction Detection in Life Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28607)
*Chuchu Wu,Zhiyin Zhou,Jingzhuo Hu,Liang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出跨辖区监管分歧检测任务：给定FDA与EMA对同一主题的要求，分类其关系为AGREE、DIVERGE或SILENT（方向性）。发布RegDivergence-101基准（101对专家标注样本，kappa=0.85），并系统比较四种基线方法，发现扁平LLM评判器（Claude Haiku）表现最佳（0.830 macro-F1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制药公司同时开发面向美国和欧盟市场的药物时，必须协调FDA与EMA各自独立发布的指南。目前这种协调由监管事务专家手工完成，成本高且易出错。需要一个自动化系统来检测两个机构要求之间的一致、分歧或缺失关系。

Method: 定义三层分类任务（AGREE/DIVERGE/SILENT），其中SILENT具有方向性（SILENT_FDA vs SILENT_EMA）。构建RegDivergence-101基准，标签基于三篇同行评审的FDA/EMA对比研究及原始监管指南文本，双重标注kappa=0.85。比较四种基线：词汇启发式、NLI交叉编码器、义务级Graph-RAG、扁平LLM评判器。

Result: 词汇启发式macro-F1为0.511（95% CI [0.411-0.605]），NLI交叉编码器仅0.233，义务级Graph-RAG为0.663（[0.570-0.747]），扁平LLM评判器/Claude Haiku最佳为0.830（[0.747-0.908]）。三个方向性发现：SILENT可被语义检测但蕴含式方法看不到；对级义务图优于词法但逊于扁平LLM上下文；语料级图构建是大规模静默检测的架构目标。

Conclusion: RegDivergence-101作为试点基准，确立了任务表述和基线层级。扁平LLM评判器当前最优，但语料级图构建被指出是大规模静默检测的下一步架构方向。仍有四个未覆盖的监管领域和扩展路线图在第7节描述。

Abstract: Pharmaceutical sponsors developing a drug for both the United States and the European Union must reconcile guidance issued independently by the FDA and the EMA. Where the two agencies require substantively the same thing, a sponsor can file once; where they diverge, a single trial design risks rejection in one region; where one agency is silent on a point the other regulates, the sponsor must infer obligations. Today this reconciliation is performed manually by regulatory-affairs experts. We introduce cross-jurisdiction regulatory divergence detection: given an FDA requirement and an EMA requirement on the same topic, classify their relationship as AGREE, DIVERGE, or SILENT. SILENT is inherently directional (SILENT_FDA vs. SILENT_EMA); we record direction per pair and report per-direction F1 alongside the collapsed label. We release RegDivergence-101, a 101-pair expert-grounded pilot evaluation benchmark (labels grounded in three peer-reviewed FDA/EMA comparison studies and primary FDA/EMA/ICH guidance text; dual-annotation inter-annotator kappa = 0.85), and systematically characterise a four-method baseline hierarchy: lexical heuristic (0.511 macro-F1, 95% CI [0.411-0.605]), NLI cross-encoder (0.233), obligation-level Graph-RAG (0.663 [0.570-0.747]), and flat LLM judge / Claude Haiku (0.830 [0.747-0.908]). Three directional observations emerge at pilot scale (n = 101): SILENT is semantically detectable but invisible to entailment-only formulations; pair-level obligation graphs improve over lexical methods but trail flat-LLM context (CIs partially overlapping); and corpus-level graph construction is the indicated architectural target for large-scale silent-detection.
