*Li Li,Tong Zhang,Wentao Yu,Zuobin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次引入熵空间理论，通过拓扑结构覆盖深度学习模型的所有可能性，并证明其是一个赋范空间。基于该理论提出统一坐标系，可为模型各状态进行坐标化并按最大信息熵压缩值排序，为深度学习提供新的数学基础先验框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在理论上长期滞后于实践，作者希望通过建立熵空间理论来使深度学习更易于理解和分析，弥补其数学基础方面的不足。

Method: 通过拓扑结构构建熵空间，该空间独立于网络参数；设计基本运算和范数，在形式公理框架下证明熵空间是赋范空间；在此基础上提出统一坐标系，用于坐标化模型状态并按最大信息熵的压缩值进行排序。

Result: 成功证明了熵空间在形式公理框架下是一个赋范空间，并建立了能够对深度学习模型所有状态进行坐标化和排序的统一坐标系。

Conclusion: 熵空间理论为深度学习的数学基础提供了新颖的先验框架，有望缩小理论与实践之间的差距。

Abstract: Deep learning is often criticized for its theoretical research lagging behind practice. To make deep learning easier to understand, the entropy space theory is first introduced here. The entropy space can cover all the possibilities of any deep learning model by topological structure. It is independent of network parameters. Through the designed fundamental operations and norm, entropy space is proven to be a normed space within the formal axiomatic framework. Based on the theory, a unified coordinate system is proposed. It can coordinatize every state of a model and rank them by compression of the maximal value of information entropy. The theory offers a novel priori framework for mathematical fundamentals of deep learning.

</details>


### [271] [MMPCBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models on Proactive Critique of Flawed Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29286)
*Jinzhe Li,Gengxu Li,Jinnan Li,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MMPCBench，一个评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）主动批判能力的综合框架，包含4大类12子类的错误分类体系、分层评估协议和对齐感知指标。在14个主流MLLM上的测试揭示了模型在主动批判方面的明显不足，并发现了\"一致性鸿沟\"现象——推理模型能在内部推理中正确识别和分析错误，却在最终输出中抑制这些正确见解以优先服从用户指令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM评估主要关注理想场景下的指令遵循能力或简单的拒绝行为，忽略了模型对错误用户输入进行主动识别、分析和修复的能力。随着MLLM进化为复杂的交互式助手，其可靠性不仅取决于遵循指令，还取决于验证指令的正确性，因此需要填补这一评估空白。

Method: 提出了MMPCBench评估框架，包含：（1）细粒度错误分类体系，涵盖从跨模态矛盾到缺失视觉前提的4大类12个子类错误；（2）分层评估协议，分别衡量模型的错误检测、诊断和解决性能；（3）对齐感知指标，用于评估内部推理与最终响应之间的一致性。并在14个主流MLLM上进行系统测试。

Result: 14个主流MLLM的测试显示模型在主动批判方面存在明显弱点，尤其在处理细微视觉异常时表现不佳。研究还发现了普遍的\"一致性鸿沟\"现象：推理模型常能正确识别和分析错误，但在最终输出中却抑制这些正确见解以优先保证响应合规性。

Conclusion: 当前MLLMs的主动批判能力不足是普遍现象，存在内部推理与最终输出之间的\"一致性鸿沟\"，说明模型在推理正确性与响应顺从性之间存在权衡问题。这一发现对提升MLLMs作为可靠交互助手的可信度具有重要启示。

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) evolve into sophisticated interactive assistants, their reliability depends not only on following instructions but also on validating them. We define Proactive Critique as the model's autonomous ability to identify, analyze and fix faulty user inputs without extra prompts. However, evaluations mainly test models under ideal circumstances or simple refusal behaviors, largely ignoring active error processing. To fill this gap, we propose MMPCBench, a comprehensive framework for evaluating MLLMs' proactive critique competence. It features a fine-grained taxonomy of 4 primary error types spanning 12 subcategories, ranging from cross-modal contradictions to missing visual premises. We adopt a hierarchical evaluation protocol to measure models' error detection, diagnosis and resolution performance, and apply alignment-aware metrics to assess the coherence between internal reasoning and final responses. Tests on 14 mainstream MLLMs show obvious weaknesses in proactive critique, especially in dealing with subtle visual anomalies. Notably, we identify a pervasive "consistency gap": reasoning models can often correctly identify and analyze errors during internal reasoning yet suppress these valid insights in final outputs to prioritize response compliance. The code and data is available at https://github.com/ALIENS32/MMPCBench.

</details>


### [272] [Accelerating Unified Multimodal Models with Core-Expansion Routing and Unified Computation Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29291)
*Wengyi Zhan,Chenqian Yan,Songwei Liu,Mingbao Lin,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CE-Router，一种基于token重要性探测的统一多模态模型加速方法。通过发现理解与生成任务间存在"核心-扩展"不对称结构，设计任务共享核心评分器与进度条件生成扩展，在推理时压缩token计算并协调层跳过、FFN剪枝、扩散头部缓存复用和去噪步提前退出，在保持98.03%密集理解性能的同时实现1.93倍端到端推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型同时支持理解与生成任务，但在token、层和生成时间步上存在大量冗余计算。现有方法未充分利用理解与生成任务间计算需求的共性，导致推理效率低下。

Method: 通过token重要性探测发现理解任务具有稳定的重要性分量（核心），而生成任务共享该分量但需要进度依赖的修正（扩展）。据此构建CE-Router，包含任务共享核心评分器和进度条件生成扩展，通过生成分解与跨任务核心对齐进行优化。推理时压缩token计算，并向量化计算调度模块提供学习路由信号，协调层跳过、FFN剪枝、扩散头部缓存复用和去噪步提前退出。

Result: 在两个代表性统一多模态架构上验证，理解和生成任务均获得一致的性能-效率提升，保留98.03%的密集理解性能，实现1.93倍端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: CE-Router通过揭示并利用统一多模态模型中理解与生成任务的"核心-扩展"计算结构，结合多层次调度策略，实现了质量与效率的良好平衡，为多模态模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models jointly support understanding and generation, but incur substantial redundant computation across tokens, layers, and generation timesteps. Through token-importance probing, we identify an asymmetric core-expansion structure: understanding exhibits a stable importance component, while generation largely shares this component but requires progress-dependent corrections. We therefore propose CE-Router, which uses a task-shared core scorer and progress-conditioned generation expansions, optimized through generation decomposition and cross-task core alignment. At inference, CE-Router compacts token computation and supplies a learned routing signal to Unified Computation Scheduling, which coordinates layer skipping, FFN pruning, diffusion-head cache reuse, and denoising-step early exit. Experiments on two representative UMM architectures demonstrate consistent quality--efficiency improvements across both tasks, retaining 98.03\% of dense understanding performance with a 1.93$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup.

</details>


### [273] [Predicting Future Organ Dysfunction in ICU Patients Using Temporal Convolutional Networks on MIMIC-IV Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29301)
*Razan Albouq,Asra Aslam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了使用时间卷积网络（TCN）从MIMIC-IV的ICU多变量时序数据预测次日SOFA评分，五折交叉验证R²为0.740，MAE为1.431，但SHAP分析表明模型主要依赖最近观测日作为严重程度锚定机制而非真正的序列模型，并通过轨迹聚类识别出改善组（58.9%）和持续重症组（41.1%）两种临床表型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU患者未来器官功能障碍的预测对早期临床干预至关重要，但现有机器学习方法多将SOFA评分作为二元死亡预测的输入特征，而非将其本身作为连续的临床结局进行预测。

Method: 使用残差TCN模型，基于MIMIC-IV中提取的多变量ICU时序数据，采用三天滑动窗口预测次日SOFA评分；使用SHAP进行可解释性分析；使用无监督轨迹聚类识别ICU住院期间的临床表型；五折交叉验证评估模型性能。

Result: 残差TCN达到五折交叉验证R²为0.740±0.013，MAE为1.431±0.022，在RMSE和R²上优于朴素持久性基线。SHAP分析表明模型主要由最近观测日主导，心血管功能障碍是横断面严重程度和急性恶化最强的判别因素。轨迹聚类识别出改善组（58.9%）和持续重症组（41.1%）两种表型，后者涉及心血管、肝脏、凝血和肾脏功能障碍。

Conclusion: TCN能够从ICU生理数据中提取有意义的预测信号，但短输入窗口和完整病例选择偏倚限制了其临床实用性，未来需要更长的输入视野、替代的缺失数据处理策略以及外部验证。

Abstract: Predicting future organ dysfunction in Intensive Care Unit (ICU) patients is critical for early clinical intervention, yet existing machine learning approaches have largely treated the Sequential Organ Failure Assessment (SOFA) score as an input to binary mortality prediction rather than as a continuous clinical outcome in its own right. We investigate the extent to which a Temporal Convolutional Net work (TCN) can predict next-day SOFA scores from multivariate ICU time-series data extracted from MIMIC-IV, characterise the relative contribution of each organ system to total SOFA variance and deterioration, and identify distinct trajectory patterns across ICU stays. A residual TCN trained on three-day sliding windows achieved a five-fold cross-validation R2 of 0.740 +- 0.013 and MAE of 1.431 +- 0.022, outperforming a naive persistence baseline on RMSE and R2. SHAP interpretability analysis revealed that the model functions primarily as a severity-anchoring mechanism rather than a true sequence model, with predictions dominated almost entirely by the most recent observation day. Cardiovascular dysfunction emerged as the strongest discriminator of both cross-sectional severity and acute deterioration, and unsupervised trajectory clustering identified two clinically meaningful phenotypes, an improving group (58.9%) and a persistently severe group (41.1%), differentiated by cardiovascular, hepatic, coagulation, and renal involvement. We conclude that TCNs can extract meaningful predictive signal from ICU physiological data, but that short input windows and complete-case selection bias currently limit their clinical utility, motivating future work on longer input horizons, alternative missing-data strategies, and external validation.

</details>


### [274] [Formal Concept Analysis with Three Types of Negation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29311)
*Zhenghua Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FCACOI框架，将矛盾否定、对立否定和中间否定三种否定类型引入形式概念分析（FCA），基于逻辑LCOI+PLCOI构建带三种否定的形式背景、Galois连接算子、形式概念和概念格，并研究属性蕴含推理与属性约简。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典FCA仅处理对象与属性之间的正向关系，缺乏处理否定的机制，无法表达矛盾、对立和中间状态等复杂否定语义。

Method: 基于包含三种否定的集合SCOI和逻辑LCOI+PLCOI，定义形式背景、Galois连接算子、形式概念与概念格；引入ICOI-蕴含作为属性推理的语义蕴含，建立与LCOI+PLCOI推理的关联；提出两种属性约简研究框架并进行比较。

Result: 通过ICOI-蕴含建立了FCACOI属性蕴含推理与LCOI+PLCOI推理之间的关联，证明LCOI+PLCOI中的推理规则对FCACOI属性蕴含推理有效；通过具体实例展示了属性蕴含推理的能力，并提出了两种属性约简框架。

Conclusion: FCACOI基于更丰富的逻辑和语义，将FCA从仅描述肯定命题的理论提升为能够同时描述肯定、矛盾、对立和中间过渡状态的理论，扩展了FCA的表达能力。

Abstract: Classic Formal Concept Analysis (FCA) primarily focuses on the positive relationships between objects and attributes and does not have mechanisms for handling negation.To overcome this limitation, we introduce three types of negation concepts (contradictory negation, opposite negation, intermediary negation) into FCA.Based on the set SCOI and logic LCOI+PLCOI with these three types negation, we define formal context, Galois connection operators, formal concept and concept lattice with three types of negation,this leads to the proposal of a FCACOI: Formal Concept Analysis with contradictory negation, opposite negation and intermediary negation.For the reasoning in FCACOI, this paper focuses on attribute implication reasoning. Based on the logic LCOI+PLCOI and its semantics, we introduce the notion of ICOI-entailment as the semantic implication for attribute implication reasoning in FCACOI. Through ICOI-entailment, a connection is established between attribute implication reasoning in FCACOI and inference in the logic LCOI+PLCOI, it indicate that formally proven inference rules (theorems) in LCOI+PLCOI are valid in the attribute implication reasoning of FCACOI, LCOI+PLCOI provides a logical foundation for attribute implication reasoning in FCACOI. To illustrate the capability of attribute implication reasoning in FCACOI, we discuss its application in a concrete example. Moreover, we explore attribute reduction of the formal context in FCACOI, propose two research frameworks for attribute reduction from different perspectives, and compare their characteristics.We believe that, based on richer logic and semantics, FCACOI elevates FCA from a theory that describes affirmations to one that can describe affirmations and its contradiction(either this or that), opposition(extreme negation) and intermediary (transitional states between oppositions).

</details>


### [275] [BIRD-History: A Benchmark for History-Driven Text-to-SQL with Fine-Grained Knowledge Annotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29345)
*Yunfan Zhou,Qiming Shi,Yizhou Yang,Di Weng,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BIRD-History基准，包含11个数据库上的1,393个任务，用于评估text-to-SQL系统利用历史SQL日志处理隐含领域知识的查询能力，并提出了一个可插拔的检索器来提取和检索历史查询中的相关知识，实验证明该方法在四个text-to-SQL系统上均取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based的text-to-SQL系统在标准基准上表现优异，但当用户查询隐含依赖领域特定知识（如业务逻辑、数据约定、分析实践）时表现不佳，而这些知识既不在schema中也不在问题中明确表述。历史SQL查询日志是这类知识的宝贵来源，但现有基准无法充分支持历史驱动方法的评估。

Method: 1) 构建BIRD-History基准，包含1,393个任务、11个数据库，每个任务标注了哪些历史查询包含相关知识以及哪些SQL子句编码了这些知识；2) 提出一个可插拔的检索器，从历史SQL脚本中提取五类外部知识，然后检索并重排序相关片段用于查询生成，可无缝集成到现有few-shot text-to-SQL流程中而无需修改提示。

Result: 在四个text-to-SQL系统上进行实验，均取得一致的性能提升，证明了利用历史查询日志处理隐含未明确指定查询的有效价值。

Conclusion: 历史SQL查询日志是解决隐含领域知识问题的有效途径，BIRD-History基准和可插拔检索器为这一方向提供了系统性的评估框架和实用工具。

Abstract: While recent Large Language Model (LLM)-based text-to-SQL systems achieve impressive performance on standard benchmarks, they struggle when user queries implicitly rely on domain-specific knowledge, such as business logic, data conventions, and analytical practices, that is neither captured by the schema nor explicitly stated in the natural language question. Historical SQL query logs offer a valuable source of such knowledge, yet existing benchmarks do not adequately support evaluation of history-driven approaches. To address this gap, we introduce BIRD-History, a benchmark consisting of 1,393 tasks across 11 databases, designed to evaluate text-to-SQL systems' ability to ground underspecified natural language questions using historical SQL scripts. Each task is annotated with ground-truth labels specifying which historical queries contain relevant knowledge and which SQL clauses encode it, enabling systematic evaluation of both retrieval effectiveness and knowledge utilization. Alongside the benchmark, we propose a plug-in retriever that extracts five types of external knowledge from historical SQL scripts, then retrieves and reranks relevant fragments for query generation. The retriever integrates seamlessly into existing few-shot text-to-SQL pipelines without requiring prompt modifications. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements across four text-to-SQL systems, highlighting the value of leveraging historical query logs for handling underspecified queries. Dataset and code are open-sourced on https://github.com/zjuidg/BIRD-History.

</details>


### [276] [Extending TotalSegmentator: Predicting Patient and Acquisition Characteristics from CT and MR Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29348)
*Jakob Wasserthal,Joshy Cyriac,Michael Bach,Kimia Mozahheb Yousefi,Minh-Son To,Máté Sik,Cédric Hémon,Thomas Weikert,Martin Segeroth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文开发并评估了基于3D ResNet-10集成的开源多任务模型，可从CT和MR图像直接预测患者特征（体重、身高、年龄、性别）和采集元数据（造影剂、椎体覆盖、图像噪声等）。在内部测试集上表现优异，CPU推理仅需20秒（CT）和12秒（MR），模型已集成到TotalSegmentator工具中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 影像归档中的患者详细信息和采集元数据常缺失或不可靠，而这些信息对临床决策、图像质量控制和自动化研究管线至关重要，因此需要一种直接从图像中快速恢复这些特征的方法。

Method: 分别训练CT和MR两个3D ResNet-10集成模型。CT模型基于57,291例临床检查训练，MR模型基于43,200例训练（2011-2025年数据）。两个模型均预测体重、身高、年龄、性别、造影剂存在、椎体覆盖和图像噪声；CT额外预测制造商、管电压、管电流、卷积核及注射后时间，MR预测序列类别。性能在内部CT（n=501）、MR（n=636）及外部CT数据集（n=54）上评估。

Result: 内部CT测试MAE为体重3.90kg、身高3.68cm、年龄4.42岁，性别F1=0.990；MR对应结果为4.34kg、4.62cm、7.13岁，F1=0.970。CNN在四个核心目标上均优于分割衍生XGBoost基线（校正P≤.042）。CT造影剂F1=0.963，MR序列F1=0.953，MR造影剂F1=0.823。外部CT MAE为4.45kg、4.05cm、5.17岁，性别F1=0.971。CPU推理CT需20秒，MR需12秒。

Conclusion: 每个模态仅需一个3D多任务模型即可快速从异质性CT和MR检查中恢复患者及采集特征，且模型已公开发布在TotalSegmentator中，具备良好的临床实用性和可及性。

Abstract: Background: Patient details and acquisition metadata are important for clinical decisions, image quality control, and automated research pipelines, but may be missing or unreliable in imaging archives.
  Purpose: To develop and evaluate a fast open-source model that predicts patient and acquisition characteristics directly from CT and MR images.
  Materials and Methods: Separate 3D ResNet-10 ensembles for CT and MR were trained on 57,291 and 43,200 clinical examinations acquired from 2011 to 2025. Both predicted weight, height, age, sex, contrast presence, vertebral coverage, and image noise. The CT model additionally predicted scanner manufacturer, tube voltage, tube current, convolution kernel, and post-injection time; the MR model predicted sequence class. Performance was evaluated on internal CT (n=501) and MR (n=636) test sets and an external CT dataset (n=54).
  Results: Internal CT MAEs were 3.90 kg, 3.68 cm, and 4.42 years for weight, height, and age, with sex F1=0.990; corresponding MR results were 4.34 kg, 4.62 cm, 7.13 years, and F1=0.970. The CNN outperformed a segmentation-derived XGBoost baseline for all four core targets in both modalities (adjusted P<=.042). F1 scores were 0.963 for CT contrast, 0.953 for MR sequence, and 0.823 for MR contrast. External CT MAEs were 4.45 kg, 4.05 cm, and 5.17 years, with sex F1=0.971. CPU inference required 20 seconds for CT and 12 seconds for MR.
  Conclusion: One 3D multitask model per modality can rapidly recover patient and acquisition characteristics from heterogeneous CT and MR examinations. Models are available in TotalSegmentator: https://github.com/wasserth/TotalSegmentator

</details>


### [277] [Cross-Relational Preference Learning for Better LLM Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29352)
*Runsheng Li,Kai Sun,Bin Shi,Bo Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CRPL框架，通过跨关系扰动和跨区域对采样来建模指令间关系，结合原子约束验证机制，构建高质量的偏好数据以提升LLM的复杂指令遵循能力，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法常依赖偏好学习来增强LLM的复杂指令遵循能力，但忽略了不同指令间可允许响应空间的关系，限制了模型对齐微妙且多样的约束变化。

Method: 提出Cross-Relational Preference Learning (CRPL)框架，包含两大关键技术：Cross-Relationship Perturbation（跨关系扰动）和Cross-Region Pair Sampling（跨区域对采样），显式建模指令间关系以生成多样的偏好数据；同时引入基于原子约束的验证机制来严格评估响应对约束的满足程度，确保偏好对构建质量。

Result: 在多种偏好学习方法（如DPO、KTO）、多种LLM主干和四个指令遵循基准上的大量实验表明，该方法相比先前基线取得显著改进，并展现出强大的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模指令间关系并引入原子约束验证，CRPL能够有效生成高质量的多样化偏好数据，显著提升LLM在复杂指令遵循方面的表现。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) still exhibit limited capability in following complex instructions. While existing approaches often rely on preference learning to enhance this ability, they typically overlook the relationships between the permissible response spaces of different instructions, which restricts a model to align with subtle and diverse constraint variations. To address this, we propose Cross-Relational Preference Learning (CRPL), a novel framework for constructing preference data that explicitly models inter-instruction relationships through two key techniques: Cross-Relationship Perturbation and Cross-Region Pair Sampling. This enables the generation of more diverse preference data that captures a wide spectrum of constraint variations. Additionally, we introduce an atomic constraint-based verification mechanism to rigorously assess response satisfaction, ensuring high-quality preference pair construction. Extensive experiments across multiple preference learning methods (e.g., DPO, KTO), LLM backbones and four instruction-following benchmarks demonstrate that our approach achieves substantial improvements over prior baselines and exhibits strong generalization.

</details>


### [278] [APPSolver: Adaptive Patch Partitioning for Point-Wise Ship Flow Prediction on Unstructured Meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29355)
*Wenhua Huo,Fenglei Han,Wangyuan Zhao,Xiao Peng,Chunhui Wang,Jialin Wu,Jiayi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APPSolver是一个基于自适应分块（APP）的船体水动力逐点流场预测框架，采用确定性四叉树对船体CFD仿真的二维水平切片进行自适应划分（近船体细密、远处稀疏）。在修正后的评估协议下，学习型分词器比APP-Transformer更准确，但持久性基线的单步MAE更低，表明APP的主要价值在于计算效率而非预测优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型非均匀点集使得直接基于注意力的代理建模在船体水动力预测中成本高昂，需要更高效的表示方式来提升计算效率。

Method: 提出APPSolver框架，包含自适应分块（APP）四叉树表示法，对固定二维水平切片进行粒度自适应划分、下采样和预测恢复。同时建立修正的评估协议，包括自然(t,t+1)配对构建、训练集归一化统计复用以及三个模型种子的报告。

Result: 在代表性DTC输入上，APP-Transformer需1.815 GFLOPs和1.309 ms前向传播；相对于均匀分区+学习切片，自适应分区将MAE降低16.4-24.9%。但持久性基线的单步MAE低于所有模型，学习型分词器优于APP-Transformer。条件编码器在留一船体评估中提供特定增益，但绝对下一状态目标无法准确建立长期动力学。

Conclusion: APP是一种紧凑的空间表示方法，具有明确的精度-效率权衡，其优势主要在计算层面而非预测准确性层面。

Abstract: Large non-uniform point sets make direct attention-based surrogate modeling costly for ship hydrodynamics. We introduce APPSolver, a point-wise flow-prediction framework built around Adaptive Patch Partitioning (APP), a deterministic quadtree representation for fixed two-dimensional horizontal slices extracted from ship CFD simulations. APP assigns finer patches near the hull and coarser patches farther away, downsamples patch contents, and recovers predictions to the full reference point set. Under a corrected protocol that constructs natural $(t,t+1)$ pairs before splitting, reuses training-set normalization statistics, and reports three model seeds, learned tokenizers are more accurate than APP-Transformer, and a persistence baseline has lower one-step MAE on all three ShipBench hulls. The supported benefit of APP is therefore computational rather than universal predictive superiority: on a representative DTC input, APP-Transformer requires 1.815 GFLOPs and 1.309 ms per model forward, while a matched ablation shows that adaptive partitioning reduces MAE by 16.4-24.9\% relative to a uniform partition augmented with learned slicing. Condition encoders provide setting-dependent gains in leave-one-hull-out evaluation, but the current absolute next-state objective does not establish accurate long-horizon dynamics. These results characterize APP as a compact spatial representation with an explicit accuracy--efficiency trade-off. Code is available at https://github.com/wenhuahuo/APPSolver .

</details>


### [279] [Plant-Inspired AI: Plants as Inspiration for Novel Problem Formulations, and Two Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29356)
*Deepayan Sanyal,Joel Michelson,Carla E. Cao,Adam B. Roddy,Maithilee Kunda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出植物智能行为可以启发新的AI框架，通过两个植物问题解决案例（Boquila三叶藤的叶片拟态和根-茎协调生长）来展示现有AI框架（如监督学习、树搜索、约束满足）未能覆盖的新型问题类型，并初步阐述了这些框架的任务公式化及其在非植物问题上的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI长期从生物智能研究中获取灵感（如强化学习源于动物学习研究），但植物智能行为尚未被充分探索。植物学家发现植物具有广泛而复杂的适应性行为，这些行为可能映射到现有AI框架无法解决的新型问题类别，因此有必要从植物智能中挖掘新的AI范式。

Method: 作者通过两个具体案例阐明观点：(1) Boquila trifoliolata的叶片拟态——该藤本植物能同时模仿多种宿主树的叶片形态，这是一个高度特异的案例；(2) 根-茎协调生长——植物在探索不同环境的器官系统间分配资源，这是大多数植物共有的行为。作者从这两个案例中提取底层计算原理，识别出当前AI尚未解决但符合这些原理的问题，并初步提出任务公式化方法。

Result: 作者识别出两组现有AI框架未覆盖的问题类型，分别对应叶片拟态（高度特异性但计算原理独特）和根-茎协调生长（跨植物普遍存在），并给出了初步的任务公式化，展示了如何将这些植物启发的框架应用于非植物领域问题。

Conclusion: 植物行为蕴含着未被AI社区充分挖掘的计算原理，有望为AI开辟新的问题解决框架，补充而不是取代现有的监督学习、树搜索和约束满足等方法。未来工作可在任务公式化基础上，将植物启发的新框架推广至更广泛的非植物问题。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) has long been inspired by studies of biological intelligence. Reinforcement learning, for instance, drew inspiration from studies involving animal learning and is now a powerful paradigm for solving many real-world problems. Recently, plant biologists have uncovered a wide range of complex behaviors in plants that enable them to flexibly adapt to variable environments. Here, we argue that such behavior can motivate new AI frameworks encompassing a range of problems overlooked by existing problem-solving frameworks such as supervised learning, tree search, and constraint satisfaction. We illustrate this idea with two examples of intelligent problem-solving in plants: (1) leaf mimicry in Boquila trifoliolata, a vine capable of altering its leaves' morphology to resemble those of multiple host trees simultaneously; and (2) coordinated root-shoot growth, wherein plants allocate resources across organ systems exploring distinct environments. While leaf mimicry is highly specific to Boquila, coordination of root-shoot growth is shared across most plants. For both examples, we capture underlying computational principles and identify problems fitting these frameworks that are currently unaddressed by AI. Finally, we outline preliminary task formulations and discuss how these formulations may be applied to non-plant problems.

</details>


### [280] [LiteSearch-VL: Small Multimodal Search Agents via Trajectory Distillation and Synthetic Step-DPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29357)
*Saeed Khaki,Nima Safaei,Kamal Ginotra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在单节点低算力预算下，将大型多模态搜索智能体的轨迹蒸馏到小型视觉语言骨干模型（Qwen3-VL-2B/4B）中时，究竟哪些能力被有效迁移。通过使用已发布的OpenSearch-VL轨迹、LoRA适配器和基于GPT-5生成硬负样本的合成DPO偏好学习，发现核心效果是"行为契约"的迁移而非均匀的准确率提升，并指出答案验证而非搜索深度是小型多模态智能体的下一个瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强大的多模态搜索智能体依赖昂贵的专有前沿模型或大规模开放视觉语言模型配合大量智能体数据训练。作者质疑：在有限算力下将已发布智能体轨迹蒸馏到小模型时，真正被迁移的是什么？这既是成本问题，也是理解知识蒸馏本质的科学问题。

Method: 提出LiteSearch-VL低计算量配方：(1) 仅使用公开的OpenSearch-VL轨迹数据；(2) 在Qwen3-VL-2B和4B上使用参数高效的LoRA适配器；(3) 针对五种局部失效模式（过早回答、错误工具、弱查询、重复查询、忽略图像）使用GPT-5生成硬负样本进行DPO合成偏好学习；(4) 通过12,400个GPT-5评判的rollouts在SimpleVQA、FVQA、LiveVQA和VDR-Bench-testmini上进行系统评估，并进行受控的VDR步骤预算消融研究。

Result: 全轨迹监督微调使2B模型从几乎不产出可用答案（1,237/1,240个no_answer）跃升至28.4%的macro Pass@1，持平甚至略超未微调的4B基线（25.6%）。合成偏好学习和紧凑工具蒸馏只是优化而非质变（最佳4B配置达30.8% Pass@1）。VDR消融显示额外搜索轮次将弃答转为错误实体错误而非正确答案，表明答案验证才是瓶颈。

Conclusion: 小型多模态搜索智能体的主导效应是行为层级的契约迁移（学会如何使用工具产生答案），而非均匀的准确率提升。搜索深度的增加无法弥补答案验证能力的不足，未来应聚焦于小型模型的答案验证与错误检测能力。

Abstract: Multimodal search agents answer visual questions by interleaving image understanding, web retrieval, tool use, and evidence synthesis. Strong systems exist, but in two expensive regimes: proprietary frontier models such as GPT-5 and Gemini, or large open vision-language backbones trained with substantial agentic data and reinforcement learning. We ask a different question: when released agent trajectories are distilled into much smaller backbones under a single-node budget, what is actually transferred? We study this with LiteSearch-VL, a low-compute recipe for Qwen3-VL-2B and Qwen3-VL-4B that uses only released OpenSearch-VL trajectories, parameter-efficient LoRA adapters, and synthetic step-level preferences: DPO on GPT-5-generated hard negatives targeting five local failure modes (premature answer, wrong tool, weak query, repeated query, ignored image). Across 12,400 GPT-5-judged rollouts on SimpleVQA, FVQA, LiveVQA, and VDR-Bench-testmini, the dominant effect is behavioral rather than a uniform accuracy lift: full-trajectory supervised fine-tuning transfers the agent contract, taking the 2B model from almost never emitting a usable answer (1,237/1,240 no_answer rollouts) to 28.4% macro Pass@1, matching or slightly exceeding the off-the-shelf 4B base (25.6%). Synthetic preference learning and compact tool distillation act as refinements rather than phase transitions (best 4B configuration: 30.8% macro Pass@1). Finally, a controlled VDR step-budget ablation shows that extra search turns convert abstentions into wrong_entity errors rather than correct answers, identifying answer verification, not search depth, as the next bottleneck for small multimodal agents.

</details>


### [281] [TRACER: Per-Tool Context Retention for LLM Agents via Consequence-Attributed Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29363)
*Ziqi Lin,Ye Wu,Mengying Yang,Xu Liu,Yizhou Liu,Qiang Ke,Qin Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACER框架，将企业数据代理中的上下文压缩建模为逐工具的顺序决策问题。通过轻量级REINFORCE策略，根据查询条件分配保留比例，并采用后果感知目标函数同时考量任务成功率、总token消耗和压缩后的工具重调用。实验显示在三个压缩后端上相比保留全部上下文可减少29-46%的token消耗，同时保持或提高任务成功率，并可在不同代理骨干和压缩架构间迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有压缩策略在分配保留预算时未考虑移除单个工具输出带来的下游后果。激进压缩可能触发昂贵的工具重新调用，抵消初始的token节省，即所谓的"压缩-后果差距"（compression--consequence gap）。

Method: TRACER将压缩建模为逐工具的顺序决策问题：(1) 轻量级REINFORCE策略在每个压缩事件仅利用当前可用信息分配查询条件化的保留比例；(2) 后果感知目标函数联合考量任务成功、总token消耗和压缩后的工具重调用；(3) 使用学习到的结果模型比较所选保留比例与完全保留各工具输出的预测后果，以改善信用分配。

Result: 在三个压缩后端上，TRACER相比保留全部上下文减少29-46%的token消耗，同时保持或提高任务成功率；相比静态工具类型条件策略额外节省15-18%的token；在五个LOCA-bench环境上减少18-25%的token消耗，且策略可跨代理背板和压缩架构迁移。

Conclusion: 研究证明了后果感知的逐工具上下文保留策略对提升长时程语言代理效率具有重要价值，通过识别工具输出的因果后果而非单纯压缩，能够在保持任务性能的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: Enterprise data agents answer business queries by chaining many tool calls over multiple reasoning steps, routinely accumulating hundreds of thousands of context tokens per session. Existing compression strategies typically allocate retention budgets without accounting for the downstream consequences of removing individual tool outputs. Aggressive compression may therefore trigger costly tool re-invocations that offset the initial savings. We call this the compression--consequence gap. To close it, we propose TRACER, which formulates compression as a sequential per-tool decision problem. A lightweight REINFORCE policy assigns query-conditioned retention ratios using only information available at each compression event. Its consequence-aware objective jointly accounts for task success, total token consumption, and post-compression tool re-invocations. To improve credit assignment, TRACER uses a learned outcome model to compare the predicted consequences of the selected retention ratio with those of fully retaining each tool output. On held-out production queries across three compressor backends, TRACER reduces total token consumption by 29--46% relative to keeping all context while maintaining comparable or higher task success. Compared with a tool-type-conditional static policy, TRACER provides an additional 15--18% of token savings. Interventional rollouts show that the learned per-tool credit scores correlate with measured single-tool consequences. The learned policy also yields positive savings when transferred across agent backbones and compressor architectures, and reduces token consumption by 18--25% on five held-out LOCA-bench environments. These results demonstrate the value of consequence-aware, per-tool context retention for improving the efficiency of long-horizon language agents.

</details>


### [282] [Reviving our data foundations is the most disruptive step to data maturity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29368)
*Valentina Carapella,Ernesto Jimenez-Ruiz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，中小型成熟企业要充分受益于AI技术，应摆脱炒作，聚焦于建立或重建良好的知识基础层。作者建议采用低影响的数据策略，以知识图谱技术为核心，以模块化、动态和跨功能的方式进行设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业的AI应用往往被炒作所迷惑，忽视了知识基础层的建设。对于中小型成熟企业而言，向高管团队传达这一信息需要扎实的证据支持，同时需要将对现有流程的影响降到最低。

Method: 本文是一篇愿景声明（vision statement），提出重新思考什么样的证据能说服决策者，并设计一种低影响的数据策略，使其适应企业现有且不断变化的数据流和业务流程。主张以模块化、动态和跨功能的方式构建知识图谱。

Result: 作者坚信知识图谱技术将逐渐成为AI驱动型企业数据策略中不可或缺的组成部分，前提是采用模块化、动态和跨功能的设计方法。

Conclusion: 中小型成熟企业应明智地降低对AI炒作的关注，将资源投入到知识基础层的建设上；知识图谱技术是这一战略的核心要素，但需要以低影响、模块化的方式渐进实施。

Abstract: The most disruptive step that enterprises of small-medium size and maturity can take to make the most of the latest technological advances in AI is to step back from the hype and focus on establishing or reviving a good knowledge foundation layer. It is a hard message to present to the executive team; therefore, it needs to be backed by evidence, and its implementation needs to be of minimal impact on the existing processes. In this vision statement, we discuss how we need to rethink what evidence speaks to the decision-makers and propose a low-impact data strategy that adapts to the existing and ever-changing data flows and processes across the company. We firmly believe that knowledge graph techniques will increasingly become non-negotiable in the data strategy of an AI-powered enterprise, provided that we approach their design in a modular, dynamic and cross-functional way.

</details>


### [283] [FORESIGHT-9: Prospective and Process-Aware Evaluation of Adaptive Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29372)
*Xiangxin Luo,Chengtian Hong,Haohua Li,Yongyi Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了FORESIGHT-9，一个前瞻性且流程感知的交易智能体基准测试框架，通过从共同信息边界分叉出的九条可审计的反事实压力世界线来评估自适应交易智能体，不仅评估投资组合结果，还评估智能体状态与执行在替代未来中的一致性。实验发现固定等权策略在36次运行中胜过31次，且过程遥测能揭示终端收益隐藏的内部退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统回溯测试对自适应交易智能体的评估存在局限：无法排除历史污染、对单一市场路径敏感、无法揭示长期适应过程中的内部退化。现有评估方法无法全面检验智能体在多种未来情境下的真实表现和内部状态健康度。

Method: 从共同的2026年7月信息边界分叉出九条可审计的反事实压力世界线；每条世界线设定分阶段宏观金融事件和联合多资产锚点；使用确定性生成器实现轨迹，按世界内时间披露观测；通过通用契约标准化观测与执行，同时保留每个智能体的原生适应循环；评估两种自适应交易框架搭配两种基础模型骨干，共36次长期运行。

Result: 智能体排名在不同世界线和骨干模型间差异显著；固定等权策略在36次运行中优于31次；过程遥测揭示了终端收益无法暴露的失败——在一次高收益运行中，实时因子库已崩溃而实际持仓收敛到等权回退策略，但决策记录仍报告活跃的因子集成。

Conclusion: FORESIGHT-9不仅评估投资组合结果，还评估自适应智能体的状态和执行在替代未来中是否保持连贯。该基准暴露了单纯依赖终端收益评估的盲区，为自适应交易智能体的评估提供了更全面的框架。作者已发布世界线、轨迹、审计追踪和再生脚本。

Abstract: Retrospective backtests provide a limited test of adaptive trading agents: they cannot rule out historical contamination, expose sensitivity to a single realized market path, or reveal internal degeneration during long-horizon adaptation. We introduce FORESIGHT-9, a prospective and process-aware benchmark built from nine auditable counterfactual stress worldlines branching from a common July 2026 information boundary. Each worldline specifies staged macro-financial events and joint multi-asset anchors; a deterministic generator realizes the trajectories, while observations are disclosed according to in-world time. A common contract standardizes observations and execution while preserving each agent's native adaptation loop. We evaluate two adaptive trading-agent frameworks with two foundation-model backbones across 36 long-horizon runs. Agent rankings vary substantially across worldlines and backbones, and a fixed equal-weight policy outperforms 31 of 36 runs. Process telemetry exposes failures that terminal returns conceal: in one high-return run, the live factor library collapsed while executed holdings converged to the equal-weight fallback, even though decision records continued to report an active factor ensemble. FORESIGHT-9 therefore evaluates not only portfolio outcomes, but whether adaptive agent state and execution remain coherent across alternative futures. We release the worldlines, trajectories, audit traces, and regeneration scripts.

</details>


### [284] [Evaluating Tiny Recursive Models Across Training for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29376)
*Anjani Sirivella,Aanisha Newaz,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了约2800万参数的递归Transformer模型（TRM-AR）在自然语言生成Python代码任务中的性能，发现仅在teacher-forced评估下模型排名会随训练反转两次，而联合考虑拟合与生成质量并沿训练轨迹评估才能得到一致结论。在参数匹配条件下TRM-AR表现更好且抵抗过拟合，但有效深度相同时传统Transformer更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归模型通过复用单一模块增加深度以降低参数规模成本，但现有评估仅基于teacher-forced拟合或单一检查点的任务准确率，而代码生成实际是自由运行（free-running）的。teacher-forced优势能否在自由运行中保持、是否在训练过程中稳定，这个问题尚未被研究，因此需要系统的跨训练轨迹评估。

Method: 使用约2800万参数的递归自回归模型TRM-AR，与参数匹配和深度匹配的两种传统Transformer对照，在自然语言到Python代码生成任务上，跨越40个epoch和3个随机种子，同时追踪teacher-forced拟合（验证损失）和自由运行生成质量，并按验证损失选择检查点进行比较。

Result: 在递归模型与深度匹配对照组之间，拟合排名在训练过程中反转了两次。在参数相等时，TRM-AR在拟合、生成和泛化上均优于参数匹配对照，分别恢复了两个对照组之间约45%的验证损失差距和57%的生成质量差距，但每步计算成本约为参数匹配对照的175倍。在有效深度相等时，传统Transformer在验证最优点上拟合和生成均更好。

Conclusion: TRM-AR的优势主要在于抵抗过拟合而非更强的模型能力。递归代码生成模型的评估应沿训练轨迹联合考察拟合与生成质量，而非在单一检查点上评估。

Abstract: Code generation increasingly relies on large transformer models, whose capability advances with scale. Yet such a scale is costly, creating demand for small models, especially where data is limited. Recursive models address this by reusing a single block to add depth rather than stacking independent layers. Such models are typically evaluated by teacher-forced fit (next-token loss on ground-truth prefixes) or task accuracy, at a single checkpoint, whereas code is produced by free-running generation, where the model extends its own output. Whether a teacher-forced advantage survives free-running generation, and whether it holds across training, remains open. To study both, we compare a ~28M-parameter autoregressive Tiny Recursive Model (TRM-AR) on natural-language-to-Python code generation against parameter-matched and depth-matched controls, tracking fit and generation across 40 epochs and three seeds. The fit ranking between the recursive model and the depth-matched control reverses twice. Selecting each checkpoint by validation loss and examining the trajectory yields a consistent comparison. At equal parameters, TRM-AR fits, generates, and generalizes better than the parameter-matched control while recovering approximately 45% of the validation-loss gap and 57% of the generation-quality gap between the two controls, at roughly 175 times the per-step cost of the parameter-matched control. However, at equal effective depth, the larger transformer fits and generates better at its validation optimum, suggesting TRM-AR's advantage lies in resistance to overfitting, not greater capability. These findings suggest that recursive code generation models should be evaluated jointly on fit and generation across the training trajectory rather than at a single checkpoint.

</details>


### [285] [EvoGenUI-Bench: Evaluating LLMs as Multi-Turn Generative UI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29387)
*Yue Peng,Lanke Xia,Zihan Wang,Jiahao Ye,Ke Ning,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EvoGenUI-Bench基准，用于评测大语言模型在多轮交互中维护可执行网页界面的能力，包含750个回合和三种场景，发现最强模型在五轮完整任务中仅完成37.3%，揭示了生成式UI评估需要从单轮输出转向跨轮状态同步测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽能生成交互式网页界面，但可靠生成式UI需要在用户需求不断演变时维持可执行工件。现有评估缺乏对多轮界面维护能力的系统评测。

Method: 构建EvoGenUI-Bench基准，包含150个五轮任务共750个回合，覆盖三种场景（信息展示、可执行交互、工具锚定的外部状态）；在浏览器中执行生成工件，通过截图、源码与DOM证据、执行者轨迹和运行时日志进行评测；提出Adjacent Pass Retention（APR）指标衡量跨轮保留能力。

Result: 在八个模型中，最强的仅达到74.9%的回合通过率，完成五轮完整任务的只有37.3%；工具锚定任务上APR降至52.4%。诊断分析显示：展示类失败集中于信息架构，交互类失败源于派生状态传播和可供性绑定，工具锚定类还涉及外部状态锚定和需求分解问题。

Conclusion: 生成式UI评估应从判断孤立输出转变为测试界面行为、派生状态、外部状态和助手声明在工件演变过程中的同步性，这为生成式界面研究提供了新的评测范式。

Abstract: Large language models can generate interactive web interfaces, but reliable generative UI requires maintaining an executable artifact as user requests evolve. We introduce EvoGenUI-Bench, a benchmark for multi-turn interface maintenance comprising 150 five-turn tasks and 750 turns across three scenarios: information presentation, executable interaction, and tool-grounded external state. We execute generated artifacts in a browser and evaluate them using screenshots, source and DOM evidence, actor traces, and runtime logs. Beyond turn-level and episode-level success, we measure cross-turn retention with Adjacent Pass Retention. Across eight models, even the strongest achieves 74.9% Turn Pass while completing only 37.3% of five-turn episodes; APR further falls to 52.4% on tool-grounded tasks. Diagnostic analysis shows that presentation failures center on information architecture, interaction failures on derived-state propagation and affordance binding, and tool-grounded failures additionally involve external-state grounding and requirement decomposition. These results reframe generative UI evaluation from judging isolated outputs to testing whether interface behavior, derived state, external state, and assistant claims remain synchronized as the artifact evolves.

</details>


### [286] [Toward Latent Language Model Skills Steering and Optimization: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29459)
*Xunyi Jiang,Junda Wu,Yuxin Xiong,Sheldon Yu,Tong Yu,David Arbour,Ritwik Sinha,Julian McAuley,Hongyi Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究发现，大型语言模型（LLM）的程序性技能可以在激活空间中表示为方向向量，并且可以通过向量空间操作（如方向组合、对比学习、优化轨迹）直接操纵这些技能，从而支持了技能在表示层面的潜在向量空间组织观点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将LLM技能视为通过提示或程序表达的显式表层结构，但未回答技能在模型内部究竟如何表示，以及能否作为潜在空间中的结构化对象进行操作。作者希望探究程序性技能是否能够以向量方向的形式存在并被操纵。

Method: 这是一项实证研究。作者在激活空间中提取独立的技能方向向量，通过激活这些方向来改变模型行为；测试独立提取的方向是否能够组合形成更高级技能；使用对比方向实现上下文条件下的算法个性化；并研究技能方向上的优化轨迹演变。

Result: 研究发现：程序性技能确实具有向量空间表示；单个技能方向可被激活以改变模型行为；独立提取的方向可以组合为更高层次的技能；对比方向可以实现上下文条件下的算法个性化；技能方向的优化轨迹呈非单调演变，中间状态往往优于完全优化的解。

Conclusion: 这些结果支持了一种表示层面的程序性LLM技能观点：技能在潜在空间中具有向量级组织形式，允许通过内部干预直接操纵，为理解和控制LLM的程序性能力提供了新的视角和手段。

Abstract: Skills, as a useful abstraction for the procedural capabilities of large language models (LLMs), capture how models perform structured, multi-step reasoning and program execution. Existing approaches typically treat skills as explicit, surface-level constructs specified through prompts or programs, leaving open the question of how such procedural capabilities are represented inside the model and whether they can be manipulated as structured objects in latent space. In this empirical study, we investigate whether procedural LLM skills can be represented as directions in activation space and whether vector-space operations over these directions can express skill-level behaviors. We find that procedural skills admit a vector-space representation: individual skill directions can be activated to shift model behavior; independently extracted directions can compose to form higher-level skills. Contrastive directions yield context-conditioned algorithmic personalization and optimization trajectories over skill directions evolve non-monotonically, with intermediate states often surpassing fully optimized solutions. These results support a representation-level view of procedural LLM skills: they admit a latent vector-space organization that allows direct manipulation through internal interventions.

</details>


### [287] [Can escalation channels redirect reward hacking toward defect disclosure?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29460)
*Francesca Gomez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出通过"升级通道"（escalation channels）作为决策环境干预手段，让编码智能体在遇到有缺陷的测试基础设施时主动报告问题而非进行奖励黑客（reward hacking）。实验显示，升级工具与反奖励黑客策略的组合干预可将奖励黑客率从23.6%降至5.3%，且8个模型中6个完全消除该行为，同时升级通道还能作为诊断基础设施提升缺陷检测覆盖率10.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在遇到有缺陷的测试基础设施时可能会进行奖励黑客行为（如硬编码输出或篡改测试文件），这种现象已从基准测试蔓延到真实生产环境——某主要AI平台的协同多智能体入侵事件即为典型案例。作者指出，智能体检测和利用缺陷的能力，在正确的决策环境下同样可以转化为报告缺陷的能力，因此需要探索能同时减少奖励黑客并暴露基础设施缺陷的干预手段。

Method: 作者将"升级通道"（即智能体在冲突点可用的结构化报告工具）作为决策环境干预变量进行评估，采用2×2因子设计，分别检验升级工具、独立的反奖励黑客策略及其组合的贡献。实验覆盖5个模型家族的8个前沿模型，使用混合效应逻辑回归分析数据。

Result: 组合干预将奖励黑客率从23.6%降至5.3%（OR=9.2，95% CI 5.0-16.8，p<10^-12），且无性能开销，8个模型中6个完全消除该行为。升级与黑客行为近乎完全互斥（96.8%的升级不涉及黑客）。升级通道在监控基础上额外增加+10.1个百分点的缺陷检测覆盖率，且触发后准确率更高（99.4% vs 85.8%）。

Conclusion: 与"遏制类"（containment-based）方法不同，升级通道不会面临被日益增长的模型能力超越的风险，而是将智能体的能力从"利用缺陷"重新导向"披露缺陷"。该方法不仅减少奖励黑客行为，还通过将升级通道作为诊断基础设施，同时解决了触发该行为的基础设施缺陷问题。

Abstract: When coding agents encounter defective test infrastructure they may reward-hack: hardcoding outputs or editing test files to pass tests they cannot legitimately satisfy, a pattern that has now appeared outside benchmarks, in a coordinated multi-agent intrusion of a major AI platform's production infrastructure. The same capability that lets an agent detect and exploit a defect could let it report one, given the right decision environment. We evaluate escalation channels, structured reporting tools available to the agent at the point of conflict, as a decision-environment intervention that both reduces reward hacking and surfaces the infrastructure defects that trigger it. A $2 \times 2$ factorial separates the contributions of an escalation tool, a standalone anti-reward-hacking policy, and their combination. Across 8 frontier models spanning 5 families, the combined intervention reduces reward hacking from 23.6\% to 5.3\% (mixed-effects logistic OR = 9.2, 95\% CI 5.0--16.8, $p < 10^{-12}$) with no detectable cost or performance overhead, eliminating it entirely for 6 of 8 models. Escalation and hacking are near-perfectly mutually exclusive, with 96.8\% of escalations involving no hacking. Beyond reduction, escalation channels function as diagnostic infrastructure: on top of monitoring, escalation adds +10.1 percentage points of defect detection coverage and is more accurate once it fires (99.4\% vs 85.8\%). Unlike containment-based approaches that risk outpacing growing model capabilities, escalation channels redirect capability toward disclosure rather than exploitation.

</details>


### [288] [Call Neighbours Yourself: Graph Walks with Destination-Conditioned On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29588)
*Yilun Liu,Boyu Luo,Yanran Tang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CNY框架，允许LLM在文本属性图推理中通过拓扑约束的图行走动作主动探索邻居节点，而非在固定上下文中推理，并利用目的地条件下的在线自我蒸馏来解决邻居探索的延迟信用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在生成前就固定了可访问的邻居集合，迫使推理在静态上下文中进行，无法在推理过程中获取缺失的证据。作者认为邻居选择本身应当成为推理过程的一部分。

Method: CNY框架通过暴露轻量级邻居预览并学习何时扩展候选邻居来获取额外证据，同时引入目的地条件下的在线策略自我蒸馏（destination-conditioned on-policy self-distillation），在邻居内容揭示后回顾性地评估所选邻居，并将其导致的动作偏好变化转化为动作级训练信号。

Result: 在标准TAG推理基准的统一原文设置下，CNY持续优于固定上下文的后训练基线方法；学习到的探索策略可以迁移到未见图和训练中未遇到的图级任务。

Conclusion: 邻居选择应成为推理过程的一部分，主动探索图邻居比静态上下文推理更有效，且学到的探索策略具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Reasoning over text-attributed graphs (TAGs) requires large language models (LLMs) to combine a node's text with evidence distributed across its neighbourhood. Existing methods fix the set of accessible neighbours before generation, forcing reasoning to operate over a static context and preventing the model from acquiring missing evidence during inference. We argue that neighbour selection should itself be part of the reasoning process. To this end, we propose Call Neighbours Yourself (CNY), a framework that enables LLMs to proactively explore graph neighbourhoods through topology-constrained graph-walk actions. Instead of reasoning over a pre-selected neighbour set, CNY exposes lightweight neighbour previews and learns when to expand candidate neighbours for additional evidence. To address the delayed-credit challenge of neighbour exploration, we introduce destination-conditioned on-policy self-distillation, which retrospectively evaluates a selected neighbour after its content is revealed and converts the resulting change in action preference into an action-level training signal. Experiments on standard TAG reasoning benchmarks under a unified raw-text setting show that CNY consistently outperforms fixed-context post-training baselines. Furthermore, the learned exploration policy transfers to unseen graphs and to a graph-level task not encountered during training. Code is available at https://github.com/superallen13/CNY.

</details>


### [289] [Not Safe for All: Auditing the Dialect Penalty in Text-to-Image Safety Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29589)
*Minkyu Kim,Juhwan Choi,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了文生图(T2I)安全护栏对非标准英语方言存在系统性公平性失败，将其形式化为"方言惩罚"(dialect penalty)，并通过23,080对提示词的跨5种方言评测、受控错别字消融实验及分组平衡重训练方案，证明了该偏差源于训练数据不平衡且可通过平衡数据暴露而非GroupDRO目标来缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I安全护栏的评估通常基于标准英语，忽视了不同方言使用者可能遭受的不公平对待。作者希望探究护栏是否因方言的语言学表面特征而非语义意图触发误判，从而揭示被平均准确率基准掩盖的公平性缺陷。

Method: 构建23,080对跨5种英语方言的提示词对数据集，系统评测文本级过滤器和像素级生成器的方言表现；通过错别字消融实验区分方言特有偏差与通用分布外敏感性；通过训练数据不平衡分析定位偏差来源，并采用分组平衡重训练与GroupDRO(组分布鲁棒优化)对比消融验证缓解策略的有效性。

Result: 文本级过滤器在相反方向失败：NSFW-T过度标记良性方言提示、LatentGuard过度标记有毒提示（偏差差距最高+28.29个百分点），而OpenAI Moderation API则欠检测。错别字消融确认惩罚源于方言特征标记而非通用分布外敏感性。像素级生成器基本不受方言影响，惩罚在文本处理阶段引入并级联到事后护栏。分组平衡重训练可缓解偏差，且增益归因于平衡暴露而非GroupDRO的最差组目标。

Conclusion: 当前T2I管线对非标准方言使用者存在系统性公平性失败，这是被平均准确率基准掩盖的公平性缺陷。该偏差可归因于训练数据不平衡，并可通过分组平衡重训练有效缓解，提示社区应关注数据层面的公平而非仅依赖鲁棒优化算法。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) safety guardrails fail to generalize equitably to non-standard dialects. Evaluating 23,080 paired prompts across five English dialects, we formalize this failure as the dialect penalty, where filters trigger based on linguistic surface features rather than semantic intent. Text-level filters fail in opposing directions: NSFW-T over-flags benign dialect prompts and LatentGuard over-flags toxic ones (bias gaps up to +28.29 pp), while the OpenAI Moderation API under-detects them. A controlled typo ablation confirms this penalty originates from flagging dialectal features, not generic out-of-distribution sensitivity. The pixel-level generator is largely dialect-agnostic; the penalty enters at text processing and cascades unevenly to post-hoc guardrails. We show this bias tracks training data imbalance and is mitigable via group-balanced retraining, with an ablation attributing the gain to balanced exposure rather than to the worst-group objective of GroupDRO (group distributionally robust optimization). Current pipelines systematically fail dialect speakers, an equity failure masked by mean accuracy benchmarks. Our official code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/minguinho26/dialect-penalty-t2i.
  Content Warning: This paper contains offensive, toxic, or disturbing text prompts and generated images.

</details>


### [290] [Towards a Systems Foundation for Agentic Skills: Architecture, Lifecycle, and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29596)
*Sanket Badhe,Deep Shah,Priyanka Tiwari,Nehal Kathrotia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了"智能体技能（agentic skills）"作为构建大规模自主语言智能体的统一系统基础范式，建立了涵盖九个阶段生命周期的参考架构，并探讨了市场动态、安全威胁、防御机制及各领域的系统实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体在复杂长时程任务中面临可靠性、上下文消耗和执行稳定性等瓶颈，传统的单体提示工程和无状态工具调用范式难以扩展，亟需模块化、可复用、可移植的智能体技能范式来支撑规模化部署。

Method: 将技能形式化为外部化程序知识，连接高层认知规划与确定性执行环境；系统性地构建九阶段生命周期架构（自主发现、编写与表示格式、记忆存储、动态检索与路由、组合与编排、执行与修复、终身适应、经验评估、安全治理），并分析市场生态、公共注册表及对抗性威胁向量。

Result: 对软件工程、操作系统导航、具身机器人、科学发现等领域的系统实现进行了分类梳理，识别出持续学习和基准真实感等关键开放挑战，为构建可扩展、鲁棒且可验证的自主语言智能体提供了基础性框架。

Conclusion: 智能体技能应当被确立为基础性范式，其模块化外部知识表达方式能够有效解决现有智能体扩展瓶颈，是实现规模化、稳健且可验证的自主语言智能体的关键路径。

Abstract: Autonomous large language model (LLM) agents increasingly face reliability, context consumption, and execution stability bottlenecks when deployed on complex, long-horizon tasks. While monolithic prompt engineering and stateless tool-calling paradigms struggle to scale, the field is rapidly converging toward \emph{agentic skills}: modular procedural abstractions that externalize execution knowledge into reusable, executable, and portable artifacts. This paper establishes a unified systems foundation and reference architecture for the agentic skills ecosystem. We formalize skills as externalized procedural knowledge bridging high-level cognitive planning with deterministic execution environments, and systematically delineate the architecture across a nine-stage lifecycle: autonomous discovery, authoring and representation formats, memory storage, dynamic retrieval and routing, composition and orchestration, execution and repair, lifelong adaptation, empirical evaluation, and security governance. We further examine marketplace dynamics, public registries, and emerging adversarial threat vectors, alongside runtime verification and defense mechanisms. Finally, we categorize system implementations across software engineering, operating system navigation, embodied robotics, and scientific discovery, while highlighting critical open challenges in continual learning and benchmark realism. This work establishes agentic skills as a foundational paradigm for building scalable, robust, and verifiable autonomous language agents.

</details>


### [291] [LLMs Interpret, Embeddings Organize, Graphs Emerge: Agent-Driven Compilation of Scientific Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29612)
*Shi-Ju Ran,Kun Zhang,Xi Wu,Liu-Si Yang,Wen-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一个名为ASKS（Agent-Driven Scientific Knowledge System，智能体驱动的科学知识系统）的科学知识编译系统，通过LLM将每个来源转化为可读的Wiki视图和机器可理解的语义，再通过确定性检查和嵌入几何将更新整合到持久知识状态中。作者在56篇论文上进行了验证，展示了来源可追溯的研究画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续的科学工作需要一个能够跨任务承载解释并保留到源证据路径的知识基础。现有的知识管理系统难以在积累知识的同时维持与原始来源的可追溯性，因此需要一种自动化的科学知识编译过程。

Method: ASKS系统流程：1) LLM为每个来源生成可读Wiki视图和机器语义；2) 确定性检查将语义转化为文档级GraphDelta；3) 嵌入几何结合显式图规则将变更整合到持久状态；4) 每次摄入都是可检查的状态转换，编译后的Wiki和图视图均链接到保留的源记录。

Result: 按时间顺序编译了同一研究项目的56篇已发表论文。分析表明：分支存活率、跨论文支持、谱系、覆盖率和变动率形成了以张量网络方法为中心、分支涵盖量子多体研究、张量网络机器学习和量子AI方向的来源可追溯研究画像。Hub组织保持稳定且低变动，规范节点增长以增量为主。

Conclusion: ASKS系统能够实现来源可追溯的科学知识编译，图级测量和导航路径都保留与来源记录的链接，验证了该方法的有效性，为长期积累和解释科学研究提供了一个可检查的知识基础设施。

Abstract: Sustained scientific work requires a knowledge substrate that carries interpretation across tasks and preserves paths to source evidence. We call this process \emph{scientific knowledge compilation} and implement it in ASKS, the \emph{Agent-Driven Scientific Knowledge System}. For each source, an LLM produces a readable Wiki view and machine-facing semantics. Deterministic checks convert the latter into a document-local GraphDelta, and embedding geometry together with explicit graph rules integrates the proposed changes into persistent state. Each ingest is an inspectable state transition over accumulated knowledge, with compiled Wiki and graph views linked to the preserved source record. We examine this process by chronologically compiling 56 published papers from one research program. Branch survival, cross-paper support, lineage, coverage, and churn yield a source-traceable author research portrait centered on tensor-network methods, with branches into quantum many-body research, tensor-network machine learning, and quantum-AI-oriented directions. In this run, higher-level Hub organization remains stable and low-churn. Canonical-node growth is predominantly additive. Graph-level measurements and navigation paths retain links to the source records from which they were compiled.

</details>


### [292] [Detect Before You Attribute: Cascade Failure Attribution for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29646)
*Jiayi Zhang,Zexin Wang,Degang Sun,Changhua Pei,Fei Sun,Gaogang Xie,Jingjing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DUOTRACE，一个即插即用的检测过滤器，用于提升LLM智能体失败归因的准确性。它采用"先检测后归因"的范式，通过VAE异常检测识别异常执行节点，并向LLM归因方法提供聚焦的轨迹证据，在六个基线方法上将智能体级和步骤级归因准确率分别提升8.7%和7.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在执行复杂任务时容易失败，准确的失败归因对提升智能体可靠性至关重要。现有基于拓扑和频谱的方法忽视了细粒度语义，而基于LLM的归因方法在长轨迹上存在长上下文退化问题。

Method: DUOTRACE遵循先检测后归因的范式，包含以下核心技术：1）双重视图语义-结构节点表示，融合语义和结构信息；2）基于Tree-LSTM的轨迹编码器，处理层级执行结构；3）前缀链和LLM辅助数据增强，解决异质节点、层级结构和失败数据不足的问题；4）VAE异常检测识别异常执行节点，为下游LLM归因提供聚焦证据。

Result: 在六个LLM归因基线上，DUOTRACE分别将智能体级和步骤级归因准确率提升了8.7%和7.0%。

Conclusion: 通过先检测后归因的策略，DUOTRACE有效缓解了长轨迹上下文退化问题，显著提升了LLM智能体失败归因的准确性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents have shown strong potential in solving complex tasks through multi-step reasoning, yet they remain vulnerable to execution failures. Accurate failure attribution is therefore critical for improving agent reliability. Existing topology- and spectrum-based methods exploit trajectory structures but often overlook fine-grained semantics, while LLM-based attribution methods capture semantic cues but suffer from long-context degradation over lengthy trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose DUOTRACE, a plug-and-play detection filter for LLM-based failure attribution. DUOTRACE follows a detect-before-attribute paradigm: it first detects anomalous executions and then supplies focused trajectory evidence to downstream LLM-based attribution methods. For effective VAE-based anomaly detection on agent trajectories, DUOTRACE integrates dual-view semantic-structural node representations, a Tree-LSTM-based trajectory encoder, and prefix-chain- and LLM-based data augmentation to handle heterogeneous nodes, hierarchical execution structures, and limited failure data. Experiments with six LLM-based attribution baselines show that DUOTRACE improves agent-level and step-level attribution accuracy by 8.7% and 7.0%, respectively.

</details>


### [293] [Ideation Arena: Evaluating LLM Generated Research Ideas with Battle-style Human Expert Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29696)
*Zhiyu Chen,Keyu Zhao,Jigao Fu,Dong Liang,Yanbiao Wu,Jiaoyang Li,Haidong Xue,Xinhua Zeng,Yuanyi Zhen,Fengli Xu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ideation Arena，一个基于成对人类评估的研究创意竞技平台，评估了14个前沿LLM和5种研究智能体架构产生的创意，收集了105位研究者的6000多组双盲比较，构建了Elo排行榜，并验证了排名稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM生成的研究创意难以评估，因为其科学价值无法仅靠客观标准衡量，也没有唯一参考答案来定义何为"好创意"。现有自动评估方法与人类专家偏好存在显著差距。

Method: 构建Ideation Arena平台，采用战斗风格（battle style）的成对人工评估方法；为所有LLM和智能体提供共享的封闭文献上下文以保持公平起点；收集双盲成对比较数据，使用Elo评分构建排行榜，并通过评估者间一致性和稳健性分析验证排名；进一步构建Ideation Arena Eval基准测试自动评估器与人类偏好的一致性。

Result: 收集了105位计算机科学研究者的6000多组双盲比较；排行榜在注释者组成和领域覆盖变化下保持稳定；不同智能体框架的效果差异显著，部分框架提升了创意质量，有些则效果甚微甚至不如基础模型；最佳自动评判器仅达到72.56%的软准确率。

Conclusion: 当前LLM评判器仍无法可靠复现专家在创意评估中的偏好，表明研究创意评估仍需要人类参与；Ideation Arena提供了一个可复现的基准平台和排行榜，为未来研究创意自动评估的发展奠定基础。

Abstract: Evaluating research ideas generated by LLMs is difficult because their scientific value cannot be fully determined by objective criteria, and no single reference answer specifies what counts as a good idea. To address this challenge, we introduce Ideation Arena, a battle style platform that evaluates research ideas through pairwise human assessment. Ideation Arena evaluates ideas generated by 14 frontier LLMs and 5 research agent architectures built on 2 base models. To ensure a common starting point, Ideation Arena builds shared literature contexts from papers familiar to the participating researchers and provides the same contexts to all LLMs and agents. We collect over 6,000 double blind pairwise comparisons from 105 active computer science researchers and construct an Elo rating leaderboard of proposal-stage expert preferences in computer science under a shared closed-context protocol. We validate the rankings through interrater agreement and robustness analyses, showing that the leaderboard remains stable under changes in annotator composition and domain coverage. Our results show substantial variation in agent effectiveness, with some frameworks improving ideation quality over their backbones and others offering little benefit or even underperforming their base models. We further construct Ideation Arena Eval, a benchmark for assessing whether automated evaluators align with human preferences in research ideation. Experiments with current LLM judges show that they still cannot reliably reproduce expert preferences, with the best judge reaching 72.56% Soft Accuracy on Overall Quality. Our code, data, and leaderboards are available at https://github.com/foss12138/Research-Ideation-Arena.

</details>


### [294] [PAGE-RAG: Provenance-Aware Graph Evidence Promotion for Fixed-Budget Multi-hop Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29753)
*Haokun Deng,Xunkai Li,Hongchao Qin,Rong-Hua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PAGE-RAG提出一种基于图结构的查询感知候选筛选层，通过构建查询局部图、记录候选间连接关系并将其视作支持假设而非事实，利用溯源感知的图证据提升机制来弥合"连接-支持"差距，在多个多跳QA基准上显著提升支持F1和答案F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答RAG中，窄检索可能遗漏关键跳，宽检索则会引入主题干扰项；现有方法没有很好地利用候选之间的关系结构进行查询感知的筛选，存在"连接-支持差距"（connected candidates不一定支持答案）的问题。

Method: PAGE-RAG将图作为临时选择结构（而非假设图知识库），构建查询局部图，将候选连接记录为支持假设。采用溯源感知图证据提升方法，综合评估路径的相关性、溯源元数据、特异性、枢纽性、噪声和连贯性信号，并通过最小充分选择将支持性事实提升为紧凑的阅读器上下文。既可作为完整的检索-阅读流水线，也可作为即插即用模块插入现有RAG系统。

Result: 在三个多跳QA基准上，相同最终预算下，PAGE-RAG相较于强检索器在加权平均上提升了支持F1 10.4分、答案F1 3.3分；作为即插即用模块，进一步提升了所有报告的RAG后端（包括推理导向、压缩式、基于图、文档/块级系统）的性能。

Conclusion: PAGE-RAG证明了以图为临时选择结构、结合溯源感知信号的证据提升方法能够有效解决多跳RAG中的检索取舍难题，且其即插即用特性使其可广泛适用于各类RAG框架。

Abstract: Multi-hop question answering in retrieval-augmented gener?ation (RAG) often benefits from retrieving beyond the few candidates that will finally be read: narrow retrieval can miss an indispensable hop, while expanded retrieval introduces topical distractors. This challenge is not tied to a particu?lar knowledge-base format. Candidate pools may come from standalone retrievers, standard RAG backends, or graph-based retrieval pipelines. What is needed is a query-aware selection layer that can use relational structure to filter candidates be?fore generation. PAGE-RAG addresses this setting by using a graph as a temporary selection structure, rather than assum?ing a graph-structured knowledge base. It builds a query-local graph over retrieved candidates, records why candidates are connected, and treats each connection as a support hypothe?sis rather than support itself. We identify the resulting failure mode as a connectivity-support gap: connected candidates do not necessarily support the answer. We propose PAGE-RAG, a Provenance-Aware Graph Evidence promotion method that scores candidate paths with relevance, source-tracing meta?data, specificity, hubness, noise, and coherence signals, and applies minimal sufficient selection to promote supporting facts into a compact reader context. PAGE-RAG can serve as a complete retrieval-to-reading pipeline, and the same promo?tion stage can be inserted after existing retrieval or RAG sys?tems without replacing their upstream retrieval logic. Across three multi-hop QA benchmarks under the same final bud?get, PAGE-RAG improves support F1 and answer F1 by 10.4 and 3.3 points on a weighted average over a strong retriever. As a plug-in, PAGE-RAG further improves all reported RAG backends, including reasoning-oriented, compression-based, graph-based, and document/chunk-level systems.

</details>


### [295] [FRAMEWORKERS: A Dynamic Multi-Agent Framework for AI-Generated Video Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29814)
*Zhendong Li,Lei Sun,Letian Shi,Deheng Zhang,Ruibo Ming,Mengshun Hu,Dannong Xu,Jian Wang,Danda Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Jinjin Gu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FRAMEWORKERS，一个以任务为中心、以工作空间为基础的多智能体框架，用于开放式的视频制作。中央导演智能体通过动态维护任务栈来决定下一步执行哪个子任务和调用哪个子智能体，助理智能体负责在工作空间中执行具体任务并持久化结果。通过SFT和GRPO训练导演的路由能力，在路由准确性、故障恢复、泛化能力和端到端视频质量上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成系统依赖刚性管线，难以适应多样化的输入和动态工作流；通用大语言模型在长时程编排和多模态资产路由方面不可靠。需要一种能动态管理复杂视频制作流程的框架。

Method: FRAMEWORKERS采用双智能体架构：中央Director通过持续编辑Task Stack进行动态任务管理和子智能体调度；Assistant作为执行层，基于共享Workspace检索资产、调用子智能体并持久化产物。子智能体通过注册描述符暴露能力，支持即插即用。Director通过SFT+GRPO进行描述符条件下的任务路由微调优化。

Result: 实验表明FRAMEWORKERS在路由准确性上超越强LLM规划器，能可靠地从运行时故障中恢复，无需重训练即可泛化到未见过的子智能体，并在端到端视频质量和任务覆盖范围上优于固定管线、单智能体系统和先前的多智能体方法。

Conclusion: FRAMEWORKERS验证了任务中心化+工作空间接地+模块化子智能体的多智能体框架能够有效解决开放式视频制作中的动态编排问题，为长时程多模态内容生产提供了一种可扩展、可恢复、可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern video generators excel at synthesizing individual clips, but complete video production requires coordinating a long sequence of interdependent creative steps, including scripting, storyboarding, generation, and editing. It further demands persistent asset management and dynamic task orchestration as intermediate outputs, dependencies, and execution states evolve over time. Existing automated systems typically rely on rigid pipelines that are difficult to adapt to diverse inputs and changing workflows, while general-purpose large language models (LLMs) remain unreliable for long-horizon orchestration and multimodal asset routing. We introduce FRAMEWORKERS, a task-centric and workspace-grounded multi-agent framework for open-ended video production. A central Director formulates video creation as dynamic task management, continuously editing a Task Stack to determine which subtask to execute next and which sub-agent to invoke. An Assistant serves as the execution layer, grounding each selected task in a shared Workspace, retrieving the required assets and context, invoking the assigned sub-agent, and persisting the resulting artifacts. Execution capabilities are exposed through modular sub-agents with registered descriptors, allowing new sub-agents to be integrated without redesigning the orchestration workflow. To improve orchestration reliability, we fine-tune the Director via supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for descriptor-conditioned task routing. Experiments show that FRAMEWORKERS outperforms strong LLM planners in routing accuracy, recovers reliably from runtime failures, generalizes to unseen sub-agents without retraining, and achieves higher end-to-end video quality and broader task coverage than fixed pipelines, single-agent systems, and prior multi-agent approaches.

</details>


### [296] [Perceive to Hypothesize, Verify to Ground: An Agentic Reasoning Framework for Open-World Geo-Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29880)
*Yutian Jiang,Ruijie Li,Sisuo Lyu,Xixuan Hao,Qingxiang Liu,Yongzi Yu,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoPAVE框架，将开放世界地理定位重新定义为"先感知后验证"的推理问题，通过双层智能体架构（感知生成假设+验证证据锚定）来缓解视觉语言模型中的感知幻觉和上下文漂移问题，并引入PAVED数据集用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大视觉语言模型在开放世界地理定位任务中虽然具备强大的多模态推理能力，但缺乏显式的、基于证据锚定的验证机制，导致感知幻觉（perceptual hallucination）和上下文漂移（context drift）问题，影响定位准确性。

Method: 提出GeoPAVE（Geo-localization Perception-and-Verification-Engine），一个双层智能体框架：(1) 感知层通过单次rollout生成候选假设；(2) 验证层基于外部知识进行证据锚定，并执行三种决策动作——支持（support）、反驳（refute）和精炼（refine）。同时构建PAVED数据集，源自真实用户签到数据，包含多跳查询、多轮工具调用及结构化感知-验证轨迹。

Result: 引入了全新的PAVED基准数据集，该数据集基于真实世界用户签到数据构建，配备完整的推理轨迹（多跳查询、多轮工具调用、感知-验证结构），为地理定位的证据锚定推理提供了标准化的评估资源。

Conclusion: 通过将地理定位重塑为类人的"感知-验证"推理过程，GeoPAVE为缓解多模态模型在该任务中的幻觉和漂移问题提供了系统性方案，数据集和代码已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Open-world geo-localization requires models to reason over ambiguous visual cues through multi-step reasoning and external knowledge grounding. While recent large vision-language models exhibit strong multimodal reasoning capabilities, existing approaches still suffer from perceptual hallucination and context drift due to the lack of explicit evidence-grounded verification. In this work, we reformulate geo-localization as a human-like perceive-then-verify reasoning problem and propose GeoPAVE (Geo-localization Perception-and-Verification-Engine), a bi-level agentic framework that contains perception-based hypothesis generation via single-pass rollouts and verification-based evidence grounding for decision actions: support, refute, and refine. To support rigorous evaluation, we further introduce PAVED, a novel dataset derived from real-world user check-in data, equipped with comprehensive reasoning trajectories featuring multi-hop queries, multi-round tool invocations, and structured perception-verification traces. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/Arandinglv/GeoPAVE.

</details>


### [297] [On the Instance Hardness as a Decision Criterion in TinyML Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29913)
*Tobiasz Puslecki,Krzysztof Walkowiak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将树深度剪枝实例难度方法应用于TinyML系统，通过控制阈值来调节分类精度与能耗之间的平衡，初步结果显示此方法能以有限的分类质量损失换取能耗的显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统规模和计算需求不断增长，需要开发环境可持续的方法来降低推理过程中的计算成本和能耗，即使是在资源受限的小型设备上（TinyML系统）也是如此。

Method: 将树深度剪枝实例难度方法（tree depth prune instance hardness）创新性地应用于TinyML系统，通过控制阈值来调整分类准确率，从而影响推理的计算复杂度和能耗。

Result: 初步实验结果表明，阈值控制可以改变能耗，同时仅带来有限的分类质量变化，验证了该方法作为概念验证的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法展示了通过调节分类精度来影响计算复杂度和能耗的可行路径，为TinyML系统的节能减排提供了新思路，但当前仍处于工作进展阶段，属于初步结果验证。

Abstract: TinyML includes the implementation of machine learning on devices with limited memory and computing resources. With the development of technology, AI systems continue to scale in terms of size and computational requirements. This forces researchers to adapt methods to be environmentally sustainable by designing techniques for reducing computational costs and energy consumption in inferring AI models, even in small devices. In this work, we present preliminary findings on a novel application of the tree depth prune instance hardness method to the TinyML system. The results indicate that threshold control can change energy consumption with limited classification quality changes. This method allows us to adjust classification accuracy, thereby influencing computational complexity and energy consumption for inference. We present a work in progress with initial results as a proof of concept.

</details>


### [298] [AcrossWAM1.0:A Modular Latent World-Action Stack for Compact Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29937)
*Yafei Zhang,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AcrossWAM1.0是对潜在世界-动作模型的模块化与缩放研究，将LaWAM中耦合的世界模型、多模态骨干和部署检查点解耦为明确的模块边界，并验证了用更小骨干（Qwen3.5-0.8B）替代2B模型可在LIBERO任务上保持性能（97.45% vs 98.00%），同时将部署参数减少42.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LaWAM虽然确立了潜在世界动作模型（通过预测特征空间中的动作相关视觉子目标来避免渲染未来像素）的范式，但其原始设计将世界模型、多模态骨干和部署检查点紧密耦合，缺乏模块化边界，导致模型难以独立扩展、替换组件或进行可审计的部署。

Method: 对潜在世界动作堆栈进行模块化重构：1）策略适配器负责产生潜动作和动作生成上下文；2）保留的潜世界解码器在当前场景中锚定预测转换；3）流匹配专家生成连续动作块。同时将训练专用教师模型与推理图分离，并提供可验证的部署导出。在2,000对LIBERO episode上，用Qwen3.5-0.8B替换Qwen3-VL-2B骨干进行对比实验，并设定预设的两点保留标准（McNemar检验）验证性能等价性。

Result: 小骨干模型达到97.45%成功率，大骨干模型为98.00%，差异仅0.55个百分点，精确McNemar检验p=0.266，满足预设的两点保留标准（虽不证明完全等价）。推理可达的紧凑检查点含1,472.6M独立参数，比原2B策略少42.4%，且所有保留张量与源检查点逐位一致。另用MiniCPM-V适配器进行了跨家族冒烟测试。

Conclusion: AcrossWAM1.0提供了一个可审计的软件和评估边界，使紧凑的潜在世界动作策略可以模块化地缩放和部署，与LaWAM原始的潜子目标贡献形成互补。跨家族闭环比分的实际部署验证仍是开放性评估问题。

Abstract: Latent world-action models avoid rendering future pixels by predicting an action-relevant visual subgoal in feature space. LaWAM established this formulation, but its original presentation left the world model, multimodal backbone, and deployment checkpoint tightly coupled. We introduce AcrossWAM1.0, a modularization and scaling study of this latent world-action stack. Rather than presenting latent subgoals as a new algorithm, we make the module boundary explicit: a policy adapter produces latent-action and action-generation contexts; a retained latent world decoder grounds the predicted transition in the current scene;and a flow-matching expert generates continuous action chunks. We further separate training-only teachers from the inference graph and provide a verifiable deployment export. On 2,000 paired LIBERO episodes, replacing a Qwen3-VL-2B backbone with Qwen3.5-0.8B yields 97.45% success versus 98.00% for the 2B model (a-0.55percentage-point difference; exact McNemarp=0.266). This does not prove equivalence, but it meets a prespecified two-point retention criterion. The compact, inference-reachable checkpoint contains 1,472.6M unique parameters, 42.4% fewer than the original 2B policy, while all retained tensors are bitwise identical to the source checkpoint. Cross-family execution is additionally checked with a MiniCPM-V adapter smoke test; closed-loop cross-family transfer remains an open evaluation. AcrossWAM1.0 therefore contributes an auditable software and evaluation boundary for compact latent world-action policies, distinct from LaWAM's original latent-subgoal contribution.

</details>


### [299] [Spatial Matryoshka Training for Multi-Granularity Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29951)
*Trishan Singha Roy,Arkadeep Acharya,Vishwajeet Kumar,Jaydeep Sen,Sachindra Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ColSNAP是一种训练方法，通过对patch嵌入进行空间嵌套平均池化，生成多级压缩的层级结构，使单一模型在一次编码中支持多种压缩级别的检索，从而在索引时灵活配置精度-存储权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态late-interaction检索器在视觉丰富文档上表现优异，但每个页面以patch嵌入表示并在token级别匹配，导致存储成本高昂。现有的压缩方法在索引时固定单一压缩级别，缺乏灵活性。

Method: ColSNAP通过对backbone的patch网格进行空间池化，将patch嵌入聚合为逐渐粗糙的层级（tiers），并同时训练所有层级。每个层级对应不同的压缩级别，模型在一次前向传播（编码）中即可获得所有层级。

Result: ColSNAP训练的模型在显著压缩下仍能保持接近全分辨率检索性能；该方法可有效迁移到多个late-interaction backbone上；其主要性能提升来自对预训练检索器的轻量级适配阶段。

Conclusion: ColSNAP实现了在索引时按需配置精度-存储权衡的能力，无需在训练时固定压缩级别，为多模态检索系统提供了更灵活高效的存储方案。

Abstract: Multi-modal late-interaction retrievers achieve strong retrieval on visually rich documents by representing each page as per patch embeddings and matching at the token level. However, this approach incurs high storage costs. Existing compression methods typically fix a single compression level at indexing time, limiting flexibility. We present ColSNAP (Spatial Nested Average Pooling)1, a training method that generates a nested hierarchy of compression levels directly from a backbone's patch grid. By spatially pooling patch embeddings into pro- gressively coarser tiers and training all tiers simultaneously, a single model learns to support retrieval at multiple compression levels without architectural changes. Crucially, a single encoding pass yields every tier, enabling the accuracy-storage trade-off to be configured at indexing time to match avail- able storage budgets, rather than being fixed during training. We demonstrate that models trained using ColSNAP maintain near full-resolution retrieval performance under substantial compression and that ColSNAP transfers effectively across multiple late-interaction backbones, and achieves most of its improvements via a lightweight adaptation stage applied to a pre-trained retriever.

</details>


### [300] [SearchWiki: Learning to Build and Navigate Knowledge Wikis for Active Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29953)
*Guransh Singh,Vishwajeet Kumar,Arkadeep Acharya,Adnan Qidwai,Jaydeep Sen,Sachindra Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SearchWiki是一个检索增强生成框架，将语料库转化为分层、类型化、可导航的wiki结构（文档概览、跨文档主题页、页面级源记录），并训练WikiResearcher-9B智能体通过多轮工具调用进行信息检索。在ViDoRe-V3、FinanceBench及多个记忆基准上，该RL调优的9B模型显著优于同规模未训练基线，甚至超越或匹敌更大的外部模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的扁平化检索增强生成将语料库视为文档块的集合，忽略了文档层级结构和跨文档关系，导致检索效率和信息完整性受限。

Method: 1) 构建SearchWiki框架，将语料库合成为三层wiki结构：文档概览、跨文档主题页和页面级源记录；2) 支持逐级细化的渐进式检索（初次查找失败时可进一步精化）；3) 使用on-policy强化学习优化智能体的导航策略，采用多组件奖励函数平衡答案正确性、检索质量和轨迹效率；4) 基于Qwen 9B模型进行RL调优得到WikiResearcher-9B。

Result: 在ViDoRe-V3（8个领域）、FinanceBench和多个记忆基准（LoCoMo、LongMemEval、PersonaMem-v2）上的评估显示，WikiResearcher-9B显著优于同规模未训练基线，并超过或匹敌更大的外部模型。

Conclusion: 在结构化语料库上进行学习导航是优于扁平化检索的替代方案，证明了将检索转化为智能体多轮工具使用过程的有效性。

Abstract: Flat retrieval-augmented generation treats a corpus as a bag of chunks, discarding document hierarchy and cross document structure. We introduce SearchWiki, a harness framework that synthesizes a corpus into a hierarchical, typed, navigable wiki and trains an agent, WikiResearcher-9B, to retrieve information through multi-turn tool use. The wiki organizes knowledge into three layers - document overviews, cross- document topic pages, and page-level source records; enabling progressive refinement of retrieval when initial lookup misses. We optimize the agent's navigation policy with on-policy reinforcement learning with a multi-component reward function balancing answer correctness, retrieval quality and trajectory efficiency. Evaluation on ViDoRe-V3 (8 domains), FinanceBench, and memory benchmarks (LoCoMo, LongMemEval, PersonaMem-v2) shows that WikiResearcher- 9B which is our RL-tuned Qwen 9B model, significantly outperforms same-size untrained baselines and exceeds or matches larger external models. SearchWiki paired with WikiResearcher-9B demonstrates that learned navigation over structured corpora is a superior alternative to flat retrieval.

</details>


### [301] [Review Before Trust: Source-Grounded Integrity Gates for AI-Assisted Personal Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29965)
*Nora Girda,Adrian Groza*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种证据门控的信誉提升模型，用于纵向健康记录中医疗文档的结构化数据提取，通过确定性监控器验证生成数据的来源证据，防止未经支持的数据影响后续医疗计算。所有22项一致性及突变测试通过，重放的97个数值候选中72个被采纳、25个保留供人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型可将医疗文档转为结构化数据，但看似合理的输出可能缺乏源文档支持。在纵向健康记录中持久化此类未经验证的数据会带来完整性风险，可能影响后续摘要、趋势或预防性护理计算。因此需要一种机制，防止生成的数据在与源文档验证之前被授权用于下游用途。

Method: 提出证据门控的信誉提升模型：生成器产出候选数据后，由确定性监控器对照源文档进行验证。监控器仅当满足三个条件时才采纳候选：(1)源文中存在唯一支持引文；(2)相关字段出现在同一实验室行内；(3)所需的来源追踪信息得以保留。生成器不能自行批准输出，证据缺失或模糊时予以拒绝，被拒候选保留供人工审查而非静默丢弃。该模型在Medical DataCloud个人健康记录应用中实现。

Result: 22项一致性测试和突变测试全部通过。重放覆盖9份历史实验室PDF报告（含102条手动标注行），共产生97个数值候选：模式验证全部接受97个，早期数据包级证据检查接受94个，强化后的引文及行级策略采纳72个并保留25个供审查。

Conclusion: 研究评估的是系统完整性而非临床正确性或安全性。结果表明，在纵向健康记录中建立可执行的边界以防止生成声明自行授权其被复用，在技术上具有可行性。该模型为医疗AI辅助数据提取提供了可信度保障机制。

Abstract: Large language models can convert medical documents into structured data, but plausible output may still be unsupported by the source. Persisting such output in a longitudinal health record, a record that accumulates patient information over time, therefore creates an integrity risk: unverified data may influence later summaries, trends, or preventive-care computations. We introduce an evidence-gated trust-promotion model that keeps generated data provisional until a deterministic monitor verifies it against the source document. The monitor admits a candidate for a specified downstream use only when the source contains a unique supporting quotation, the relevant fields occur within the same laboratory row, and the required provenance is preserved. The generator cannot approve its own output, missing or ambiguous evidence causes refusal, and refused candidates remain available for human review rather than being silently discarded. We implement the model in Medical DataCloud, a personal health-record application, and evaluate it through automated tests and a replay of saved extraction outputs. All 22 conformance and mutation tests pass. The replay covers nine historical laboratory PDF reports containing 102 manually labelled rows. The reports produce 97 numeric candidates: schema validation accepts all 97, an earlier packet-level evidence check accepts 94, and the hardened quotation- and row-level policy admits 72 while retaining 25 for review. The study evaluates system integrity rather than clinical correctness or clinical safety. The results demonstrate the technical feasibility of an enforceable boundary that prevents generated claims from authorizing their own reuse in a longitudinal health record.

</details>


### [302] [EDGE: Engine for Deterministic Graph Evaluation through Conversation Simulation from Graph Structured DSL Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29971)
*Ram Kulathumani,Regunathan Radhakrishnan,Anupam Tripathi,Xiangbo Mao,Roshanak Omrani,Keshav Somani,Shwet Kamal Mishra,Shayna Lurya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AgentGraph（一种用领域特定语言驱动的规划器）的形式化评估方法论，通过图遍历算法穷举对话路径，回放可复现轨迹来度量智能体行为的确定性与一致性，并定义了多个新颖指标来量化可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体系统演变为复杂的多智能体编排工作流，缺乏系统性框架来度量智能体的行为一致性和确定性，这成为亟待解决的关键需求。

Method: 基于AgentGraph（一种由DSL驱动的规划器，通过动态可调的直向图表示智能体推理），利用图遍历算法穷举对话路径形成完整行为空间的评估集，系统回放可复现轨迹以比较观测输出和状态转换与DSL规范的差异，并定义新颖指标来测量响应与轨迹确定性、结构遵循性和语义一致性。

Result: 实验结果表明，使用AgentGraph和LangGraph等框架并配置显式结构节点转换的智能体，在确定性上显著优于未配置受控转换的智能体。

Conclusion: 采用显式结构化和受控节点转换的智能体编排方式能够显著提升行为确定性与一致性，本文提出的评估框架为智能体可靠性的系统化度量提供了有效工具。

Abstract: As agentic systems evolve into complex multi agent orchestration workflows, there is a growing and critical need for systematic frameworks that measures an agent's behavioral consistency and determinism. In this paper, we introduce a formal evaluation methodology that is grounded in AgentGraph, a planner powered by a domain specific language that represents agent reasoning through a dynamically adjustable directed graph. We leverage this structural formalism and utilize graph traversal algorithms that exhaustively enumerate conversational paths, forming a comprehensive evaluation set that captures the agent's complete behavioral space. We then systematically replay these reproducible trajectories to compare observed outputs and state transitions against the intended DSL specification. To quantify reliability, we define novel metrics that measure response and trajectory determinism, structural adherence and semantic consistency across both exact replays and their linguistic variants. Our system's results demonstrate that agents configured using frameworks like AgentGraph and LangGraph with explicitly structured node transitions show superior determinism over agents that are not configured with controlled transitions.

</details>


### [303] [An Open-Source, Event-Driven Pipeline for Cryptocurrency Market Data: Ingestion, Forecasting, and On-Chain Fraud Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29973)
*Basil Sajid Shaikh,Melrick Mascarenhas,Nuzhat Faiz Shaikh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个完全开源的加密货币数据处理管道，基于Apache Kafka和文件系统轮询器在普通硬件上复现云原生事件驱动架构，并利用构建好的数据市集进行比特币价格预测（对比ARIMA与LSTM）和以太坊欺诈检测（对比随机森林与梯度提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币市场产生高频多源数据，处理成本高昂，通常需要商业级流式处理和仓储基础设施。本研究旨在以完全开源、可在普通硬件上运行的方案复现云原生事件驱动系统的行为，降低数据处理门槛。

Method: 使用Apache Kafka配合文件系统监视轮询器替代托管云触发器，构建事件驱动管道：将Gemini历史数据分为小时和分钟粒度文件，通过两组独立的Kafka消费者异步摄取（一组用于审计日志，一组用于触发Spark ETL），清洗后的数据存入PostgreSQL仓库（含历史和聚合模式及资产专属数据市集）。在此基础上，用比特币数据市集比较季节性ARIMA与单层LSTM的价格预测效果，并用带工程特征增强的随机森林和梯度提升分类器对以太坊欺诈检测基准数据集进行评测。

Result: 报告了完整的管道架构、建模方法及评测指标，包括两类任务的对比结果。

Conclusion: 作者明确指出了研究的局限性：不同预测时域下比较模型的局限性，以及在静态且已标注数据集上评估欺诈检测的局限性。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency markets generate high-frequency, multi-source data that is expensive to work with unless a team already has commercial-grade streaming and warehousing infrastructure in place. This paper describes a fully open-source pipeline that reproduces the behavior of a cloud-native, event-driven system -- file arrival triggering a message, a message triggering compute -- entirely on commodity hardware, using Apache Kafka and a filesystem-watching poller in place of managed cloud triggers. The pipeline partitions historical Gemini exchange data into hourly and minutely files, ingests them asynchronously through two independently grouped Kafka consumers (one for audit logging, one for Spark-triggered ETL), and lands cleaned output in a PostgreSQL warehouse with historical and aggregated schemas plus asset-specific data marts. We use the resulting Bitcoin data mart to compare a seasonal ARIMA model against a single-layer LSTM network for price forecasting, and separately apply Random Forest and Gradient Boosting classifiers, with additional engineered features, to the public Ethereum fraud detection benchmark introduced by Farrugia et al. We report the architecture, the modeling methodology, and the resulting metrics, and we are explicit about the limitations of comparing forecasts issued at different horizons and of evaluating fraud detection on a static, already-labeled dataset.

</details>


### [304] [AutoCRAT: Within-trajectory Joint Control of Stochasticity and Compute for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29988)
*Hanjun Luo,Qiushi Liu,Jingya Zhang,Haihong Pang,Jiaheng Wen,Yifei Ma,Yu Yao,Chengxi Zhang,Hanrong Zhang,Yankai Chen,Hanan Salam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoCRAT，一种面向冻结大语言模型的解码端控制器，在单个推理轨迹内联合调节采样随机性和推理预算，以替代静态的一刀切推理策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
